Full gate: 11064 passed, 15 skipped (delta +21 vs Wave 41 baseline 11043 — over the +12 acceptance-criteria target by 9; matrix parametrization fan-out is the source). 1 known xdist flake (`test_knowledge_store::test_auto_commit_after_debounce` — same Wave 8/14/15/16/19/22/27/30/31/32/33/37 environmental git-debounce pattern; passes serial; unrelated to this AD). No hard-stops triggered: no decomposer-side modification (wrapper transparent at storage boundary), no audit emission (deferred AD-692b), no federation bridging to AD-679 (deferred AD-692c), no per-department matching (deferred AD-692b), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed all 17 anchors at HEAD `fdb71b5`: `_CLASSIFICATION_LEVELS` taxonomy `records_store.py:27` + `KnowledgeEdge` 13-field shape `edges.py:73-96` + `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` Protocol `:133` + `find_edges`/`traverse` signatures `:149/159` + `RecallTier` str-Enum `earned_agency.py:53` + 3 clearance helpers `:100/131/149` + runtime adoption ordering `runtime.py:1614/1618/1620/1651` + `Oracle.query` `agent_id` kwarg `oracle_service.py:167` + `_query_graph` `:480` + `_wire_nl_graph_query` sibling shape `finalize.py:394` + cascade slot `:730` + `EdgeBackfillConfig` insertion anchor `config.py:1806/2147` + `knowledge/__init__.py` re-export pattern + federation has NO edge export at HEAD + AD-679 SHIPPED orthogonal at `mesh/disclosure.py:15`). Closes GH issue #386.

AD-691 v1 CLOSED. NL-to-Graph Query Service — Ship's Computer structural routing (GH issue #385, Wave 41). Phase B (Intelligence) of the Unified Knowledge Graph stack — closes the **read seam** by adding an LLM-driven 2-phase NL→graph entry point on top of AD-687 KnowledgeEdgeStore + AD-688 Oracle Tier 6. New `src/probos/cognitive/nl_graph_query.py` with `NLGraphQueryService(runtime, *, default_max_hops=2, default_limit=10, llm_tier="standard", extraction_max_tokens=600, synthesis_max_tokens=800)` (mirrors AD-661 DiagnosticContextService runtime-injection sibling shape; reaches `runtime.knowledge_edges` + `runtime.llm_client` lazily; NEVER raises into caller — every failure path returns a well-formed degraded `NLGraphQueryResult`). Two frozen result dataclasses: `EntityExtraction(id, type)` + `to_dict()`; `NLGraphQueryResult(query, extracted_entities, edges_traversed, paths, answer, provenance)` + `to_dict()` (deep-copy semantics on list fields; non-defaulted `query` first per frozen-dc field-order rule). Single public `async query(natural_language, *, max_hops=None, limit=None) -> NLGraphQueryResult`. **Phase 1 (extraction)**: `tier="standard"` LLM call with strict-JSON system prompt → `{entities: [{id, type}], relation_filter: [...], intent: "find|traverse|count"}`. **Graph step**: per entity, calls `runtime.knowledge_edges.find_edges(source_id|target_id, ..., limit)` for direct hits (proximity 1.0) AND `traverse(source_type, source_id, max_hops, relation_filter)` for multi-hop paths. Hop-proximity formula extends AD-688: `0.6 ** (hop - 1)` → direct=1.0, hop-2=0.6, hop-3=0.36 (inline `_GRAPH_HOP_PROXIMITY_BASE` constant — NOT config; same precedent as AD-688's `_GRAPH_HOP_PROXIMITY_DIRECT/TWO_HOP`). Score = `edge.weight × edge.confidence × hop_proximity`; dedupe by `edge.id` keeping the highest-scoring instance. **Phase 2 (synthesis)**: `tier="standard"` LLM call with edge JSON inlined; system prompt mandates `[graph: <edge.id>]` citation per fact. **`provenance` field** = deduped, in-order edge IDs ACTUALLY cited in the answer (extracted via `_CITATION_RE = r"\[graph:\s*([0-9a-fA-F]{16,})\s*\]"` and filtered against `allowed_ids = {e.id for e in edges}` so hallucinated IDs are silently dropped) — distinct from `edges_traversed` which lists every edge the graph step retrieved. Four short-circuit paths: (1) `edge_store is None or llm_client is None` → `"Knowledge graph or LLM unavailable."`; (2) Phase-1 LLM exception or unparseable JSON → `"Could not parse query."` + no Phase-2; (3) `entities=[]` → `"No graph entities identified in query."` + no Phase-2 + no graph step; (4) graph step returns 0 edges → `"No relevant graph evidence."` + no Phase-2. `relation_filter` coercion: filter raw strings against `KnowledgeRelationType.value` set; unknown values dropped at `logger.debug`; empty post-coerce → pass `None` to `traverse()` (else trivially returns `[]`). `max_hops` clamped to `MAX_HOPS_CEILING=3`; `limit` floored at 1. New `NLGraphQueryConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`, `default_max_hops=2`, `default_limit=10`, `llm_tier="standard"`, `extraction_max_tokens=600`, `synthesis_max_tokens=800`) with two `field_validator`s (`default_max_hops` in [1,3], `default_limit >= 1`). Default-True deviation from Wave-10 transitional-flag convention is documented in config docstring — service is callable read-only aggregator with no automatic invocation; same precedent as `DiagnosticContextConfig`/`KnowledgeEdgesConfig`. Wired onto `SystemConfig.nl_graph_query` via `Field(default_factory=NLGraphQueryConfig)` immediately after `diagnostic_context` field. Sync `_wire_nl_graph_query(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at `startup/finalize.py` mirrors `_wire_diagnostic_context` shape exactly; invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_diagnostic_context`. Public attribute `runtime.nl_graph_query` (Wave 5 conv #1; collision-free greenfield — 0 hits at HEAD `98ecbb8`). New `src/probos/routers/nl_graph_query.py` with `GET /api/nl-graph-query?q=&max_hops=&limit=` — 400 on empty `q`, 503 when service None, hard caps `max_hops` to [1,3] and `limit` to [1,100]; returns `result.to_dict()`. Registered alphabetically into `api.py` import + for-loop tuples (twin-block SEARCH/REPLACE bundled into single multi_replace per Wave 31/33 pattern). **Decomposer integration via `NLGraphQueryAgent`** (Section 5b) — new `src/probos/agents/utility/nl_graph_query_agent.py` ships a single-agent utility pool wrapping the service. Pattern mirror: `agents/introspect.py` (`tier="utility"`, `_runtime`-backed delegation, full `perceive→decide→act→report` lifecycle, `requires_reflect=True`, no consensus). Declares `IntentDescriptor(name="nl_graph_query", tier="utility", requires_consensus=False, requires_reflect=True)` so the decomposer's existing dynamic IntentDescriptor discovery (cognitive/decomposer.py:280 — confirms NO hardcoded "ask" route) routes structural-query natural language to it WITHOUT any decomposer-side modification. Agent params: `query` (natural-language question), `max_hops` (optional, 1-3), `limit` (optional). Tier-2 log-and-degrade on delegation failure. Exported from `agents/utility/__init__.py`. Spawner template registered in `runtime.py` immediately after `introspect`; pool created in `startup/agent_fleet.py` immediately after the `introspect` pool block, gated on `config.nl_graph_query.enabled` AND `runtime.nl_graph_query is not None` (third clause means pool creation cleanly skips if startup ordering ever flips, no crash). **What this AD does NOT change** (out of scope by design): no decomposer-side modification (the agent + pool route via existing dynamic registry); no embedding-based fuzzy entity match (deferred AD-691c if surfaced; v1 uses verbatim entity IDs as extracted by the LLM); no write/mutation queries (graph is read-only here); no classification-aware filtering (AD-692 commercial owns); no Fabric IQ NL2GQL parity (v1 is simple typed-triple traversal); no HXI surface (deferred until consumer signal); no new EventType (telemetry via `logger.info`/`logger.debug` v1); no Pydantic config for hop-proximity formula (inline `_GRAPH_HOP_PROXIMITY_BASE`); no persistence of query history (each call stateless); no streaming response. **Builder drift-fix during build**: (1) `BaseAgent` is abstract with required `report()` method — added `async def report(self, result)` returning `result` to NLGraphQueryAgent (mirrors `IntrospectionAgent.report` shape). (2) Test 16 referenced `from probos.spawner import AgentSpawner` (wrong path); corrected to `from probos.substrate.spawner import AgentSpawner` + `from probos.substrate.registry import AgentRegistry` and pass registry to ctor. (3) `tests/test_utility_agents.py::test_all_agents_exported` asserts `set(__all__) == expected` (exact equality) — extended `expected` to include `"NLGraphQueryAgent"` to keep the existing utility-export contract. 16 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad691_nl_graph_query.py` (over 14 floor by 2): `NLGraphQueryResult` frozen + `to_dict` round-trip; service ctor 6-kwarg shape + defaults; happy path with `_StubLLM` (Phase-1 entity → 1 direct edge → Phase-2 with citation → `provenance == [edge.id]`); Phase-1 parse failure (`llm.calls == 1`, no Phase-2); empty extraction short-circuit (`llm.calls == 1`, `find_calls == []`); `relation_filter` passthrough (coerced to `[KnowledgeRelationType.REPORTS_TO]`); unknown relation dropped silently; `max_hops=99` clamped to 3; `limit=5` truncates 25-edge result; scoring orders by `weight × confidence × proximity` descending; hallucinated `[graph: deadbeef...]` citation dropped from `provenance`; no edges → `"No relevant graph evidence."` + no Phase-2; API endpoint 400 on empty `q`; API endpoint 200 happy-path with `TestClient` + `dependency_overrides`; `NLGraphQueryAgent.handle_intent` happy-path delegates to `runtime.nl_graph_query.query(...)` with NL passed through verbatim + result fields projected to `IntentResult.result` dict (answer/provenance/extracted_entities/edge_count/path_count); pool/template registration smoke (config defaults match, IntentDescriptor declared with `tier="utility"`, spawner template registers cleanly). Phantom-API pre-check: only intro-not-yet-in-index FPs (`runtime.nl_graph_query`, `service.query()` kwargs, `NLGraphQueryAgent` lifecycle methods, module-level constants — all introduced by Sections 1–5b). Full gate: 11042 passed, 15 skipped, 1 known xdist flake (`test_dreaming.py::test_nl_to_dream_cycle_changes_weights` — passes serial in 25s; same Waves 23/27/30/31/32/33/39 pattern; unrelated to this AD). Test-count delta: +14 vs Wave 40 baseline 11028 (exact target hit). No hard-stops triggered: no decomposer-side modification (agent + pool covers the route), no fuzzy entity match (deferred AD-691c), no classification gating (deferred AD-692 commercial), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed all 17 anchors at HEAD `98ecbb8`: `KnowledgeEdge` 13-field frozen dc + 8 entity types + 10 relations + `KnowledgeEdgeStorage.find_edges`/`traverse` kwargs + `MAX_HOPS_CEILING=3` + `runtime.knowledge_edges` slot + `OracleService` Tier 6 sibling + `_GRAPH_HOP_PROXIMITY_DIRECT/TWO_HOP` + `LLMRequest`/`LLMResponse` shapes + `BaseLLMClient.complete` + `extract_json` + `_StubLLM` test pattern + `DiagnosticContextConfig` sibling shape + `SystemConfig.diagnostic_context` insertion site + `_wire_diagnostic_context` sibling shape + `routers/diagnostic_context.py` sibling shape + `api.py` twin-block + decomposer dynamic intent discovery + `IntrospectionAgent` agent pattern + `runtime.spawner.register_template("introspect", ...)` anchor + `agent_fleet.py` introspect pool block). Closes GH issue #385.

AD-690 v1 CLOSED. Dream Step 10 — Relationship Inference (GH issue #384, Wave 40). Phase B (Intelligence) of the Unified Knowledge Graph stack — first dream step that learns AGENT→AGENT edges from observed co-participation, complementing AD-689 structural backfill. New `src/probos/knowledge/rejection_cache.py` (`RejectionCacheStorage` runtime-checkable Protocol + `SQLiteRejectionCache` concrete impl mirroring `SQLiteKnowledgeEdgeStore` start/stop lifecycle; `dream_step10_rejections` table with composite PK `(source_id, target_id)`; `was_rejected` undirected — checks both `(a,b)` and `(b,a)`; `record_rejection` is `INSERT OR REPLACE` append-only). New `src/probos/cognitive/relationship_inference.py` pure service (no runtime access; deps passed in): `RelationshipInferenceResult` frozen dataclass (4 counters + `to_dict()`), `_extract_agent_pairs(episodes) -> list[tuple[str, str]]` (sorted-tuple dedup via `dict.fromkeys`, in-episode self-pair skip, singleton-agent_ids skip), `_classify_pair_with_llm(a, b, *, llm_client)` returning `(relation, confidence, rationale_or_reason)` with five rejection paths (`llm_call_failure` / `llm_parse_failure` / `llm_returned_null` / `relation_not_in_whitelist` / non-string relation), and `infer_relationships_from_episodes(*, episodes, knowledge_edges, llm_client, rejection_cache, max_pairs_per_run=50, max_inferences_per_entity=5, min_confidence=0.6)` orchestrator. Five anti-contamination guards: (1) per-run pair cap; (2) rejection-cache replay-skip BEFORE LLM call (silent — does not increment `relationship_pairs_rejected` on second pass; that counter is the first-classify rejection counter only); (3) existing-edge skip in either direction via `find_edges(source_id, target_id, limit=1)`; (4) per-entity cap counted as `relationship_pairs_capped` (still consumes a pair-budget slot); (5) min-confidence floor — sub-threshold pairs cached with reason `below_threshold_{conf:.2f}`. Relation whitelist v1: `REPORTS_TO` and `DEPENDS_ON` only (off-whitelist relations rejected as `relation_not_in_whitelist`). All inferred edges tagged `source_agent="dream_step10"`, `source_duty="relationship_inference"`, `weight=0.5`, `confidence` clamped to [0.0, 1.0]. **Architect call (Decision Log #1)**: GH issue title is "Dream Step 10" (Captain-facing, kept on AD title and roadmap) but live `cognitive/dreaming.py` already has Step 10 = AD-555 Notebook Quality Metrics; new step installs as **Step 7i** between 7h (AD-572 episodic-procedural bridge) and Step 8 (AD-385/539 gap detection) — semantically right (runs after procedure consolidation so episode metadata is fresh, before downstream metrics consume it). **Architect call (Decision Log #2)**: spec drafted `runtime.episodic.list_episodes(since=...)` but live `EpisodicMemory.list_episodes` (AD-689) has only `(*, limit=None)` kwargs; Step 7i consumes the already-in-scope `episodes` variable that earlier dream-cycle steps read — naturally captures "episodes considered in this cycle" without API drift. **Architect call (Decision Log #3)**: rejection cache implemented as dedicated SQLite-backed module rather than overloading `KnowledgeRelationType.CLASSIFIED_AS` with a sentinel — option (a) would pollute `knowledge_edges` with non-graph rows and confuse AD-688/AD-689 consumers. **Architect call (Decision Log #4)**: AGENT→AGENT-only v1 (most of the 10 enum relations are not semantically valid for an agent pair, e.g. MEMBER_OF is agent→department); whitelist of two relations + null option presented to LLM; off-whitelist returns counted as parse-failure-tier rejections. `DreamingConfig` extended with 4 flat fields (`relationship_inference_enabled=True`, `relationship_inference_max_pairs_per_run=50`, `relationship_inference_max_per_entity=5`, `relationship_inference_min_confidence=0.6`) — default-True deviation from Wave-10 transitional-flag convention is intentional + same precedent as `notebook_consolidation_enabled` / `KnowledgeEdgesConfig` / `EdgeBackfillConfig`: step is gated downstream on `knowledge_edges` and `rejection_cache` being non-None — both wired by `_wire_relationship_inference` which depends on `knowledge_edges` already being adopted at `runtime.py:1615`. Out-of-box: enabled but inert until wirer runs and dependencies exist. `DreamReport` extended with 3 counter fields (`inferred_relationships`, `relationship_pairs_rejected`, `relationship_pairs_capped`) — defaults to 0 so partial-report construction in dream-cycle exception branch (line ~1499) does not need updating. `DreamingEngine` extended with two private attributes (`_knowledge_edges`/`_rejection_cache`, both initialized to `None` in `__init__`) and two late-bind setters (`set_knowledge_edges` / `set_rejection_cache`); Step 7i block in `dream_cycle` between Step 7h and Step 8 — counters declared at block top so DreamReport assembly reads them whether or not Step 7i ran; uses `getattr(self, "_knowledge_edges", None)` defensively (mirrors existing `_agent_wm` pattern) so legacy tests using `object.__new__(DreamingEngine)` to bypass `__init__` (e.g. `test_dream_step_7f.py`) keep working without scaffolding edits. Late-bound dependencies, LLM-client check, and non-empty `episodes` all gate the block; tier-2 log-and-degrade on any inference failure. New `_wire_relationship_inference(*, runtime, config) -> bool` async wirer in `startup/finalize.py` mirrors `_wire_edge_backfill` shape (async because `cache.start()` is awaited); 4 short-circuit guards (config disabled → False; `knowledge_edges` not wired → False + debug; `dreaming_engine` not wired → False + debug; cache `start()` fails → warning + False); on success creates `data/rejection_cache.sqlite`, sets `runtime.rejection_cache` public attribute, calls both setters via `hasattr` guards. Invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_edge_backfill`. Public `runtime.rejection_cache: Any = None` slot added at `runtime.py:430` next to `knowledge_edges`. `knowledge/__init__.py` exports `RejectionCacheStorage` + `SQLiteRejectionCache`. **What this AD does NOT change** (out of scope by design): no new EventType (telemetry via `DreamReport` counters and `logger.debug` only — defer EventType to AD-690b if signal value emerges); no HXI surface; no backfill or migration of existing `knowledge_edges` (AD-689 owns backfill); no federation sync (AD-693); no classification gating (`KnowledgeEdge.classification` left at default `None` for inferred edges; AD-692 commercial owns ORACLE/SECRET labeling); no new entity types (AGENT→AGENT only; episode `agent_ids` is the entity source); no widening of relation whitelist beyond `REPORTS_TO`/`DEPENDS_ON` (defer AD-690b/c); no retry on `add_edge` failure (tier-2 log-and-degrade; failed pairs are NOT added to rejection cache so they retry next cycle); no scheduling change (existing `DreamScheduler` already invokes `dream_cycle`); no LLM tier escalation (v1 is `tier="standard"` only). **Builder drift-fix during build**: initial dream-step block accessed `self._knowledge_edges` directly, which broke 10 tests in `test_dream_step_7f.py` because they use `object.__new__(DreamingEngine)` to bypass `__init__` and don't set the new private attributes; switched to `getattr(self, "_knowledge_edges", None)` defensive read (same idiom as the existing `_agent_wm` lookup in the same file). 12 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad690_relationship_inference.py` (matches prompt's 10-floor by 2; over-floor by 2): `RelationshipInferenceResult` frozen + `to_dict` round-trip; `_extract_agent_pairs` dedupe + singleton-skip + 3-agent fan-out (6 agents → 4 unique pairs); existing edge skip-no-LLM-call; LLM happy path adds edge with all 4 provenance tags + correct relation/conf/weight; min-confidence filter records rejection with `below_threshold_0.40` reason; per-entity cap honored (10 partners, cap=5 → 5 inferred + 5 capped); max-pairs-per-run honored (50 candidates, cap=10 → exactly 10 LLM calls); rejection-cache replay-skip (run 1 records null rejection, run 2 short-circuits before LLM call → counters do not double-count); LLM JSON parse failure → cached with reason `llm_parse_failure`; off-whitelist relation (`member_of`) → cached with reason `relation_not_in_whitelist`; disabled config short-circuits (mirrors dream-engine guard, asserts LLM call count 0); `SQLiteRejectionCache` round-trip with `tmp_path` (start → record → was_rejected both directions True → unrelated pair False → stop). Phantom-API pre-check returns 1 candidate (`runtime.episodic.list_episodes(since=...)` in DLog #2 prose — explicitly noted in spec as the rejected captain draft; false positive). Full gate: 11028 passed, 15 skipped (delta +12 vs Wave 39 baseline 11016 — exact target hit). Pre-commit deletion sanity check: 6 modified files, all additions, 139 insertions / 0 deletions; new files `rejection_cache.py` + `relationship_inference.py` + `test_ad690_relationship_inference.py` untracked-and-tracked-fresh (no deletion risk). No hard-stops triggered: no widening to non-AGENT entity types (deferred AD-690b/c), no relation whitelist expansion (deferred), no EventType emission (deferred AD-690b), no federation sync (deferred AD-693), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed `KnowledgeEntityType.AGENT` + `KnowledgeRelationType.REPORTS_TO`/`DEPENDS_ON` + `KnowledgeEdge` 13-field frozen dc + `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` Protocol `add_edge`/`find_edges` shape + `EpisodicMemory.list_episodes` AD-689 surface + `Episode.agent_ids` field + `LLMRequest` tier/temperature/max_tokens kwargs + `extract_json` from `utils.json_extract` + `DreamingConfig` last field + `DreamReport` last field + Step 7h closing log line + Step 8 header line + `set_quality_router` setter precedent + `_reactive_cooldowns` last init line + `runtime.knowledge_edges` adoption ordering + `_wire_edge_backfill` async sibling shape + `_wire_causal_reasoner` invocation site — all 16 anchors verified at HEAD `b402fee`/Wave-39-baseline). Closes GH issue #384.

 Edge Population from Existing ProbOS Data (GH issue #383, Wave 39). Phase A foundation (fourth and last) of the Unified Knowledge Graph + Oracle Unification stack — closes the **write seam** by populating `runtime.knowledge_edges` from four already-existing data sources: ontology (REPORTS_TO + MEMBER_OF), Hebbian router (COMPETENT_IN above threshold), episodic memory (INVOLVED_IN), and DECISIONS.md cross-references (INFORMED_BY + RESOLVED_BY). Without this AD, Tier 6 (AD-688) and post-merge `_expand_via_graph` both return `[]` against an empty graph; this AD is what makes both meaningful in production. New `src/probos/knowledge/backfill.py` with `EdgeBackfillResult` frozen dataclass (per-source counts + `total` property + `to_dict()`), module-level `_deterministic_edge_id(src_type, src_id, rel, tgt_type, tgt_id) -> str` (SHA-256 of typed-triple key truncated to 32 hex chars — combined with AD-687 INSERT OR REPLACE upsert, idempotent re-runs leave row count unchanged without read-modify-write), `_make_edge` helper that bounds-clamps confidence/weight to [0.0, 1.0], and `EdgeBackfillService(*, knowledge_edges, ontology, hebbian_router, episodic_memory, decisions_paths, hebbian_threshold=0.5)` kwargs-only ctor. One method per source plus aggregator: `backfill_all() -> EdgeBackfillResult` (started_at + duration_ms timing); `backfill_ontology() -> int` (walks `get_all_assignments()` for member_of, builds `agent_by_post: dict[post_id, list[agent_type]]` index, walks `get_posts()` for reports_to chains via post → assignment(s) → agent_type resolution); `backfill_hebbian(*, threshold=None) -> int` (filters `all_weights_typed()` by `rel_type == REL_INTENT` AND `weight >= threshold`, emits `AGENT(target) COMPETENT_IN CAPABILITY(source)` since HebbianRouter REL_INTENT triples are `(intent_name, agent_id, "intent")`, confidence/weight = float(weight)); `backfill_episodes(*, limit=None) -> int` (walks new `EpisodicMemory.list_episodes` public API, emits `AGENT(agent_id) INVOLVED_IN INCIDENT(episode_id)` per agent, skips empty `agent_ids`); `backfill_decisions(*, paths=None) -> int` (parses every `### AD-NNN[a-z]?` section header in each provided markdown path, extracts `**Related:** ...` lines for INFORMED_BY edges via `r"AD-\d+[a-z]?"` regex with self-reference skip, scans body for `Closes (?:GH issue )?#NNN` markers (case-insensitive) for RESOLVED_BY edges as `DECISION:AD-N RESOLVED_BY INCIDENT:gh-N` — direction documented as architect call: source=DECISION, target=INCIDENT consistent with how relation was named in Wave 37 enum). All four backfills are tier-2 log-and-degrade — a missing/failing source contributes 0 to the count and never raises into `backfill_all()`. All edges constructed with `source_agent="edge_backfill"` + `source_duty="ontology"|"hebbian"|"episodes"|"decisions"` provenance tags. **Architect call (Decision Log #2)**: Captain spec mentioned `**Resolved by:** ...` markdown pattern but it does not exist as a structured marker in DECISIONS.md (only prose). Real marker is `Closes (?:GH issue )?#NNN`. Builder uses `Closes` as the RESOLVED_BY signal. **Architect call (Decision Log #3)**: only `REL_INTENT` weights become COMPETENT_IN edges; REL_AGENT/SOCIAL/BUILDER_VARIANT/STRATEGY out of scope v1 (would need different relations not in AD-687 enum). New public method `EpisodicMemory.list_episodes(*, limit=None) -> list[Episode]` inserted before existing `get_by_ids` in `cognitive/episodic.py` — returns episodes ordered by timestamp DESC, mirrors the existing `recent()` pattern (`:1869`), uses existing `_metadata_to_episode` reconstructor (`:2083`), `limit=None` returns all rows + non-negative int caps the slice, missing/malformed rows silently skipped. Backfill consumes this public API instead of reaching `_collection.get(...)` directly (Open/Closed; Demeter). New `EdgeBackfillConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`, `run_on_warm_boot=True`, `hebbian_threshold=0.5` validated to [0.0, 1.0] via `field_validator`, `force=False`, `decisions_paths` default to 5 markdown paths: `DECISIONS.md` + 4 era archives) wired adjacent to `KnowledgeEdgesConfig` at `config.py`. Default-True deviation from Wave-10 transitional-flag convention is intentional — same precedent as `KnowledgeEdgesConfig`/`CognitiveJournalConfig`: warm-boot wirer is a no-op once the table has any rows (idempotency-by-row-count guard), so the first cold boot after AD-689 lands populates the graph and subsequent boots see `find_edges(limit=1) != []` and skip. Async wirer `_wire_edge_backfill(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at `startup/finalize.py` mirrors `_wire_self_distillation` shape; constructs the service, sets `runtime.edge_backfill = service` (Wave 5 conv #1 — public attribute, collision-free greenfield), checks `cfg.run_on_warm_boot` short-circuit, runs idempotency guard via `await knowledge_edges.find_edges(limit=1)` (non-empty → skip + INFO log; `cfg.force=True` overrides), finally awaits `service.backfill_all()` with INFO log of per-source counts + duration. Invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_chain_optimizer` (`finalize.py:580`); phase ordering verified — `runtime.knowledge_edges` adopted in communication phase BEFORE finalize, ontology/hebbian_router/episodic_memory all attached on runtime by then. Public `runtime.edge_backfill` slot added at `runtime.py:432` immediately after `self.knowledge_edges`. Knowledge package `__init__.py` exports `EdgeBackfillResult` and `EdgeBackfillService` alongside existing AD-687 names. Layer-boundary test exception added: `("knowledge/backfill.py", "probos.mesh.routing")` — `REL_INTENT` is a pure relation-type string constant for filtering Hebbian weights (no behavioral coupling). **What this AD does NOT change** (out of scope by design): no LLM-based NL-to-graph entity extraction (deferred AD-691, issue #385); no live event-driven incremental backfill (deferred AD-690, issue #384 — Dream Step 10 inference); no classification gating on backfill writes (deferred AD-692 commercial); no federation cross-instance edge sync (deferred AD-693 commercial); no HXI graph visualization; no shell command (`/graph backfill`) — use the wirer or invoke `runtime.edge_backfill.backfill_all()` directly; no retention/pruning policy on `knowledge_edges`; no new EventType (no audit emission v1; defer to AD-690 if needed); no mutation of existing AD-687 schema or AD-688 Oracle Tier 6. **Builder drift-fix during build**: Tests #11 + #12 (warm-boot wirer happy-paths) initially failed with assertion `35 == 2` because the wirer's default `decisions_paths` includes the real `DECISIONS.md` + era archive files which exist in the test cwd; fixed by passing `decisions_paths=[]` in the test `EdgeBackfillConfig`. No shipping-code change. 12 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad689_edge_backfill.py` (over the prompt's 10-floor by 2): service shape + frozen-result + ctor surface; `backfill_ontology` emits 2 member_of + 1 reports_to with full provenance verified; `backfill_hebbian` filters below default 0.5 threshold (0.7 + 0.6 pass, 0.3 dropped, REL_AGENT skipped — capabilities sorted alphabetically); custom threshold parameter (0.7 drops both 0.4+0.6, 0.1 catches both); `backfill_episodes` emits per-agent (1 + 2 + 0 = 3 edges, ep-2 incident appears twice for alpha+beta); `backfill_decisions` parses `**Related:**` lines (3 cross-refs across 2 sections, sorted pairs verified); `backfill_decisions` parses `Closes #N` + `Closes GH issue #N` (2 RESOLVED_BY edges to gh-382/gh-383); `backfill_all` aggregation (3+1+1+2 = total 7, duration_ms ≥ 0); idempotency (2 invocations leave row count = 1, deterministic edge ID matches `_deterministic_edge_id(...)` helper output); wirer skip-when-rows-exist (pre-populated edge stays at 1 row after wirer runs); wirer runs-when-empty (1 member_of edge created from single assignment); wirer force=True override (pre-populated + force → 2 edges total). Full gate: 11015 passed, 15 skipped, 1 known xdist flake (`test_dreaming.py::test_nl_to_dream_cycle_changes_weights` — passes serial in 25.06s; same xdist concurrent-fixture-boot pattern documented in Waves 23/27/30/31/32/33 baselines; unrelated to this AD). Test-count delta: +12 vs Wave 38 baseline 11003 (exact target hit). No hard-stops triggered: no LLM extraction (deferred AD-691), no live event-driven backfill (deferred AD-690), no classification gating (deferred AD-692 commercial), no federation sync (deferred AD-693 commercial), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed `KnowledgeEdgeStorage.add_edge` INSERT OR REPLACE upsert + 8 entity types + 10 relations + ontology service public methods + `Post.reports_to`/`id`/`department_id` + `Assignment.agent_type`/`post_id` + `HebbianRouter.all_weights_typed()` + `REL_INTENT="intent"` + `Episode.id`/`agent_ids`/`timestamp` frozen dc + `_metadata_to_episode` reconstructor + `recent()` sibling pattern + `KnowledgeEdgesConfig` adjacent location + `_wire_self_distillation` async precedent + `_wire_chain_optimizer` sync precedent + `**Related:**` markdown anchors + `Closes (?:GH issue )?#NNN` markers + `### AD-NNN` section header pattern + 4 era archive files at workspace root + `runtime.knowledge_edges` adoption ordering — all 18 anchors verified live at HEAD `46fa2cd`/`bbc7fff`). Closes GH issue #383.

AD-688 v1 CLOSED. Oracle Graph Integration — Tier 6 (graph) + post-merge `_expand_via_graph` enrichment (GH issue #382, Wave 38). Phase A foundation (third of four) of the Unified Knowledge Graph + Oracle Unification stack — closes the **read seam** between AD-687 `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` and `OracleService`. Adds Tier 6 (`graph`) to `OracleService.query()` and a post-merge 1-hop graph expansion pass that enriches the top-K merged results from all tiers via the knowledge graph. New constructor kwarg `knowledge_graph: Any = None` (kwargs-only, default `None`); new public idempotent `attach_knowledge_graph(graph)` late-bind setter mirroring AD-686 `attach_semantic_layer` exactly. Wires `runtime._oracle_service.attach_knowledge_graph(runtime.knowledge_edges)` immediately after `comm.knowledge_edges` adoption at runtime.py (try/except + WARN log-and-degrade per AD-686 precedent). `"graph"` appended to default `active_tiers` list (now 6 entries: episodic/records/operational/archive/semantic/graph); existing callers passing explicit `tiers=` lists keep their narrowed behavior. **`OracleResult.provenance` is a top-level field, NOT a metadata key** — Tier 6 hits set `provenance="[knowledge graph]"`, expansion results set `provenance=f"[graph expansion: {parent.provenance}]"`, and parent provenance is mirrored into `metadata["expansion_source"]` for downstream filtering. Tier 6 entity-extraction strategy (v1): token-substring exact match — `_extract_entity_tokens(text)` lowercases + splits + strips trailing punctuation + drops <3 chars + drops ~50-token inline `_GRAPH_STOPWORDS` frozenset + dedupes preserving order + caps at 16 tokens. NOT named-entity recognition; AD-691 will add embedding-based fuzzy matching. Hop-proximity scoring: 1.0 direct (token matches edge `source_id` OR `target_id` via `find_edges`), 0.6 two-hop (one extra `traverse(max_hops=1)` step from each direct match's target). Score = `edge.weight × edge.confidence × hop_proximity`. Inline caps (NOT config): `_GRAPH_DIRECT_LIMIT=10` per direction per token, `_GRAPH_TRAVERSE_LIMIT=5` per direct match, `_GRAPH_EXPANSION_PER_PARENT=5` per top-K parent, `_GRAPH_EXPANSION_DISCOUNT=0.7`, `_GRAPH_MIN_TOKEN_LEN=3`. Dedupe by `edge.id` keeping highest score across both source/target directions and across direct/traverse merges via module-level `_record_graph_hit` helper. Empty graph or unattached graph → `[]` with `logger.debug` (mirrors AD-686 Tier-5 unattached path; never raises into `query()`). Post-merge expansion runs BEFORE the final sort/truncate so expansion results compete on score in the merged ranking; skips parents with `source_tier == "graph"` (no double-counting); reuses the same `_extract_entity_tokens` helper on `parent.content`; emits at most `_GRAPH_EXPANSION_PER_PARENT` results per parent; expansion-result score = `parent.score × 0.7 × edge.weight × edge.confidence`. Type narrowing via `Any` (mirrors AD-686 `_semantic_layer: Any`) to avoid the cognitive↔knowledge import cycle; the `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` Protocol contract is documented in method docstrings instead. **What this AD does NOT change** (out of scope by design): NL-to-graph LLM entity extraction → AD-691 (issue #385); edge population from existing data → AD-689 (issue #383); Dream Step 10 relationship inference → AD-690 (issue #384); classification enforcement on graph reads → AD-692 (issue #386, commercial); federation cross-instance edge sync → AD-693 (issue #387, commercial); HXI graph visualization → AD-690b or later; shell command (`/graph search`, `/graph traverse`) — once consumers exist; graph metrics in `oracle.stats()` — deferred (Oracle has no stats surface today). NO new EventType, NO new Pydantic config, NO new module — all edits land in `cognitive/oracle_service.py` + `runtime.py` + new test file. NO change to existing `OracleService` callers (`introspect/`, `organizer_agents.py`, `cmd_search`) — they pass explicit `tiers=["semantic"]` or rely on defaults; defaults grow but remain backward-compatible. 13 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad688_oracle_graph_integration.py` (over the prompt's 10-floor by 3): `attach_knowledge_graph` late-bind + idempotency; `_query_graph` method shape (async + kwargs-only `k`); unattached → `[]`; one-hop direct match on `source_id` (provenance + score + metadata); one-hop direct match on `target_id` (symmetry); two-hop with proximity discount (1.0 vs 0.6 verified by separate edge IDs); dedupe by `edge.id` keeps highest score (self-loop A→A matched both source and target); default `active_tiers` includes `"graph"` (AsyncMock spy verifies `_query_graph` awaited once); expansion happy path (provenance prefix `"[graph expansion: "` + score = parent×0.7×w×c + `expansion_source`/`expansion_parent_tier` metadata); expansion skips graph parents (no double-counting); expansion respects `top_k` and per-parent cap (10 parents × 20 candidate edges with `top_k=3` → first parent absorbs `_GRAPH_EXPANSION_PER_PARENT` unique edges, remaining parents see `seen_edges`); runtime late-bind smoke (SimpleNamespace shape verifies attach block); token extractor (drops short/stopwords, strips punct, dedupes, caps at 16). **Builder drift-fix during build**: prompt's `_make_edge` test helper defaulted to `KnowledgeRelationType.COLLABORATED_WITH` which does NOT exist on the AD-687 enum (verified at HEAD `102728c`: 10 actual values are REPORTS_TO/MEMBER_OF/COMPETENT_IN/RESOLVED_BY/INVOLVED_IN/INFORMED_BY/DEPENDS_ON/PRODUCED_BY/CLASSIFIED_AS/ORIGINATED_ON). Default switched to `KnowledgeRelationType.MEMBER_OF`. Pure test-fixture defect; no shipping-code change. Phantom-API pre-check: only intro-not-yet-in-index FPs (`_query_graph`, `_expand_via_graph`, `attach_knowledge_graph`, `_extract_entity_tokens`, `_record_graph_hit`, `_GRAPH_*` constants — all introduced by Sections 1–4 of this prompt). Full gate: 11004 passed, 15 skipped (delta +13 vs Wave 37 baseline 10990 — exact target hit; 13 new tests). No hard-stops triggered: no NL-to-graph extraction (deferred AD-691), no edge population (deferred AD-689), no Dream Step 10 (deferred AD-690), no classification enforcement (deferred AD-692 commercial), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed `OracleService.__init__` kwargs-only ctor + `attach_semantic_layer` mirror + `query()` dispatcher + 5-tier default + `_query_semantic` shape + `OracleResult` frozen dc with separate `provenance: str` field + `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` Protocol + `KnowledgeEdge` fields + runtime adoption ordering — all 11 anchors verified at HEAD `ef34c85`/`102728c`). Closes GH issue #382.

AD-687 v1 CLOSED. Knowledge Edge Store — typed-triple foundation of the Unified Knowledge Graph (GH issue #381, Wave 37). Phase A foundation for the Oracle Unification stack (research: docs/research/unified-knowledge-graph.md §"Storage Layer: SQLite First, Kùzu Upgrade Path"). Greenfield introduces `KnowledgeEdge` typed-triple store at `src/probos/knowledge/edges.py` with full CRUD, recursive-CTE bounded traversal (1–3 hops, hard-capped at MAX_HOPS_CEILING=3, with cycle protection via accumulated `path` column + `instr()` exclusion), Pydantic config + startup wiring, and `runtime.knowledge_edges` public attribute (Wave 5 convention #1, no underscore; collision-free verified greenfield — 0 hits at HEAD `cc3cdd3`). Cloud-Ready via the **existing** `protocols.ConnectionFactory` / `DatabaseConnection` Protocol pair (mirrors `CognitiveJournal`/AD-431, `WorkforceMemoryStore`/AD-680 — REUSED, no new connection protocol). New module ships: 8-value `KnowledgeEntityType` str-Enum (AGENT/DEPARTMENT/INCIDENT/DECISION/DUTY/FINDING/CAPABILITY/STANDING_ORDER); 10-value `KnowledgeRelationType` str-Enum (REPORTS_TO/MEMBER_OF/COMPETENT_IN/RESOLVED_BY/INVOLVED_IN/INFORMED_BY/DEPENDS_ON/PRODUCED_BY/CLASSIFIED_AS/ORIGINATED_ON); `KnowledgeEdge` frozen dataclass (5 non-default + 8 defaulted fields per Python frozen-dataclass rule) with `__post_init__` validating confidence/weight in [0.0, 1.0] and classification in {private/department/ship/fleet} (mirrors `records_store._CLASSIFICATION_LEVELS` keys, no new taxonomy); `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` runtime-checkable Protocol (6-method async surface — Dependency Inversion seam for AD-688 Oracle Tier 6 / AD-689 backfill consumers); `SQLiteKnowledgeEdgeStore` concrete impl mirroring `CognitiveJournal` shape (`__init__(db_path, connection_factory)` → async `start()` runs `executescript(_SCHEMA)` + sets `aiosqlite.Row` row_factory → async `stop()` closes; idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` + 4 indexes on source/target/relation/classification); `KnowledgeEdgeStore` public alias for default concrete store. All write methods are fire-and-forget semantically (NEVER raise to caller; sqlite3.Error → `logger.warning` + return False/[] tier-2 log-and-degrade); `update_edge` validates inputs at boundary (defense in depth, raises `ValueError` only on caller programming error pre-write). Recursive-CTE traversal: depth bound enforced via `WHERE walk.depth < ?`, cycle protection via path-string `instr()` exclusion of any target already in the accumulated `(type:id)>(type:id)` path, results grouped back into paths via `path` column rsplit on `>`, returned shortest-first. New `KnowledgeEdgesConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`, `db_path="data/knowledge_edges.sqlite"`, `max_traverse_hops=3` with `field_validator` capping at [1,3]) — DEVIATES from Wave-10 transitional-flag default-False convention with rationale documented in config docstring (boot cost = one CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS, runtime visibility zero until consumers arrive in AD-688/689/690; same precedent as `CognitiveJournalConfig`). Wired onto `SystemConfig.knowledge_edges` via `Field(default_factory=KnowledgeEdgesConfig)` immediately after `cognitive_journal` field. Startup wiring: `src/probos/startup/communication.py` constructs `SQLiteKnowledgeEdgeStore` immediately after Cognitive Journal block, calls `start()`, plumbs through `CommunicationResult.knowledge_edges` field (added next to `cognitive_journal` field; non-defaulted to match sibling shape — `SQLiteKnowledgeEdgeStore` lazy-imported under TYPE_CHECKING). Runtime adoption: `runtime.knowledge_edges: Any = None` slot added at runtime.py:425 next to `cognitive_journal` slot (Any to avoid circular import); adopted from `comm.knowledge_edges` at runtime.py:1608 next to `self.cognitive_journal = comm.cognitive_journal`. Knowledge package `__init__.py` exports the 6 new names alongside existing `KnowledgeStore`. **What this AD does NOT change** (out of scope by design — separate-issue scope, NOT v1 deferral): API endpoint (`/api/knowledge/edges/...`) → AD-688 (issue #382); Oracle Tier 6 graph integration → AD-688 (issue #382); Edge population from existing data (Hebbian/ontology/episodes) → AD-689 (issue #383); Dream Step 10 relationship inference → AD-690 (issue #384); Classification enforcement on read/write paths → AD-692 (issue #386, commercial); Federation cross-instance edge sync → AD-693 (issue #387, commercial); Kùzu migration tooling → AD-694 (issue #388, commercial); Pruning/retention policy on `knowledge_edges` (no shipped consumer in v1 → no growth signal yet); Shell command (`/edges`, `/graph`) — once consumers exist; HXI surface for graph visualisation → AD-690b or later. 12 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad687_knowledge_edge_store.py` (matches Captain's "complete v1" floor of 10 with 2-test margin): schema/migration creates table + 4 indexes; add_edge happy path returns id and persists; get_edge round-trips ALL 13 fields including custom-id; find_edges by source_type+source_id (2 of 3 edges); find_edges by relation; update_edge advances updated_at + flips confidence/weight; delete_edge with idempotent second-delete returning False; traverse 1-hop returns 2 single-edge paths; traverse 2-hop with relation_filter excludes member_of branch (only reports_to in returned edges); traverse caps at MAX_HOPS_CEILING=3 even when caller passes max_hops=5 (5-link chain → max path length 3); cycle detection — A→B→A does not infinite-loop (exactly 1 path of length 1); confidence/weight/classification bounds validation in dataclass + `KnowledgeEdgeStorage` Protocol acceptance smoke (runtime_checkable). Phantom-API pre-check returns 0 candidates (clean — every prompt-asserted symbol either lives at HEAD or is introduced by Section 1). Full gate: 10990 passed, 15 skipped + 1 known-flaky timing test (`test_knowledge_store::test_auto_commit_after_debounce` — passes serial in 9.03s; xdist concurrent git-debounce noise documented under multiple wave baselines; unrelated to this AD). Test-count delta: +12 (exact match — 11 numeric delta plus the flaky test that flips state under parallel load). No hard-stops triggered: no consumer wiring (deferred AD-688/689/690), no API surface (deferred AD-688), no classification enforcement (deferred AD-692 commercial), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed `DatabaseConnection`/`ConnectionFactory` Protocol pair at protocols.py:186/223 + `SQLiteConnectionFactory.default_factory` at storage/sqlite_factory.py:28 + `CognitiveJournal` sibling shape at cognitive/journal.py:109 + `_CLASSIFICATION_LEVELS` keys at knowledge/records_store.py:27 + `cognitive_journal` startup wiring at startup/communication.py:307 + runtime slot+adoption at runtime.py:425/1608 + `CognitiveJournalConfig` sibling at config.py:1735 — all 8 prompt-asserted live symbols verified at HEAD `cc3cdd3`). Naming-collision check (Section 0) returned 0 hits — fully greenfield. **Builder drift-fix during build**: prompt's Section 3b referenced `CommunicationServices` dataclass with defaulted `cognitive_journal` field; live class is `CommunicationResult` (results.py:151) with non-defaulted `cognitive_journal: "CognitiveJournal | None"` — added `knowledge_edges: "SQLiteKnowledgeEdgeStore | None"` (no default) immediately after to preserve dataclass field-ordering rule (defaulted-after-non-defaulted) and added TYPE_CHECKING import for `SQLiteKnowledgeEdgeStore` at top of results.py. Closes GH issue #381.

 Oracle Absorbs SemanticKnowledgeLayer (Tier 5 + 3-Consumer Migration) (GH issue #380, Wave 36). Phase A foundation of Unified Knowledge Graph + Oracle Unification stack. Adds new Tier 5 (semantic) to OracleService — non-episode ChromaDB collections (agents/skills/workflows/qa_reports/events) now flow through the same OracleResult feed as episodic/records/operational/archive. New OracleService kwarg semantic_layer= (default None, backward-compat preserved); new public OracleService.attach_semantic_layer(layer) setter for late-bind from runtime structural-services phase (Oracle is built in cognitive phase BEFORE SemanticKnowledgeLayer); new private OracleService._query_semantic(query_text, *, k, types=None) -> list[OracleResult] mirrors the existing _query_archive/_query_operational tier-method shape (degrades to [] + DEBUG log when layer not attached); `semantic` appended to default active tiers; Tier-5 dispatch block inserted between Tier-4 archive and merge-and-sort. untime._semantic_layer attribute → AD-686c (forcing function: zero remaining direct consumers — /search still uses layer.stats() for panel today); tier reordering / unifying tier name strings → AD-686d. **No SemanticKnowledgeLayer changes** (instance, write methods, stats(), lifecycle all unchanged). 11 focused tests pass at 	ests/test_ad686_oracle_semantic_tier.py (over the prompt's 7-floor by 4 — extras cover one-line-of-defence per consumer): _query_semantic method-shape (signature + ttach_semantic_layer callable + idempotent + accepts None); happy-path with stub layer (3 mixed-type results — agents/skills/workflows — normalised to OracleResult with source_tier=semantic, provenance=[semantic: <type>], metadata flattened); none-layer returns empty + DEBUG log captured; types/limit passthrough (forwarded as-is to layer.search); score coercion (None → 0.0, '0.7' → 0.7); semantic in default active tiers (invoked exactly once on tiers=None); attach late-bind (None → stub then await query returns semantic results); IntrospectionAgent._search_knowledge uses Oracle (assert_awaited_once with 	iers=['semantic'], projected dict shape carries type/id/document/score/metadata); NoteTakerAgent regression on missing-wait bug (oracle.query awaited, fetched_content contains 'Search results for'); cmd_search uses Oracle + keeps stats panel (oracle.query awaited with 	iers=['semantic'], layer.stats called once); end-to-end normalised merge (sort descending by score, semantic entries have source_tier=semantic + provenance=[semantic:...).
