AD-661 v1 CLOSED. Full Diagnostic Context for Agent Self-Improvement — PULL-BASED ASSEMBLY ONLY (GH issue #320, Wave 33). Meta-Harness research finding (Lee et al., arXiv:2603.28052): 10M tokens of raw diagnostic history beat compressed summaries — more raw context beats compressed summaries.' v1 ships a **read-only token-budgeted aggregator** over already-shipped surfaces (AD-658 chain traces + AD-657 procedure trace exemplars + EpisodicMemory.get_by_ids). NO automatic invocation, NO continuous diagnostic stream, NO semantic search, NO summary fallback, NO LLM calls, NO push-based notifications, NO AD-434 Ship's Records consumption (deferred AD-661b), NO retroactive backfill, NO bundle persistence, NO cross-bundle dedup, NO scoring/ranking beyond list_active' natural order, NO agent tier/department filtering (deferred AD-661f), NO remainder redistribution between under-filled sections (deferred AD-661c), NO HXI surface. New `src/probos/cognitive/diagnostic_context.py` with `DiagnosticBundle` frozen dataclass (query first non-default; chain_traces/procedures/episodes default_factory list, total_estimated_tokens int, truncated bool; `to_dict()` returns deep-copied list fields), three module-level helpers (`_estimate_tokens(text, *, chars_per_token=4)` heuristic `len(text) // 4` matching `agent_working_memory.py:35` precedent — no tiktoken dep; `_extract_keywords(query, *, min_len=3)` whitespace-split + lowercase + min-len drop; `_matches(text, keywords)` case-insensitive substring with empty-keywords-include-all semantics), and `DiagnosticContextService` (ctor `(runtime, *, default_budget_tokens=8000, chain_trace_ratio=0.4, procedure_ratio=0.3, episode_ratio=0.3, chars_per_token=4)` mirrors AD-659 ChainOptimizer / AD-660 CausalReasoner sibling shape). Single public async method `assemble(*, query, budget_tokens=None, agent_id=None, since=None) -> DiagnosticBundle` — never raises (each collector wrapped in try/except → `logger.warning(AD-661: ...', exc_info=True)` tier-2 log-and-degrade; failure in one section yields empty section, not failed bundle). Allocation: 40/30/30 split via section budgets `int(budget * ratio)` with episode section absorbing int-truncation remainder. Three private collectors: `_collect_chain_traces` (calls `runtime.cognitive_journal.get_recent_chain_traces(limit=200, agent_id, since=since.timestamp())`, keyword-matches across `step_name`+`sub_task_type`+`intent`+`error_truncated`+`communication_context` joined haystack, budget-clips with `truncated=True` flag); `_collect_procedures` (walks `runtime.procedure_store.list_active()` summaries → `store.get(id)` for full Procedure → resolves `trace_exemplars` via `runtime.episodic_memory.get_by_ids()`, builds entry dict with deduped `exemplar_episodes` inline + cross-procedure `exemplar_index` for episode section); `_collect_episodes` (deduped exemplars across all in-bundle procedures — explicitly NOT calling `EpisodicMemory.recall()` which would be semantic search). Token cost via `_row_tokens(row)` serializing values to single string then `_estimate_tokens`. New `DiagnosticContextConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled: bool = True` — DEVIATION from Wave-10 transitional-flag convention, documented in config docstring + prompt body: v1 is read-only aggregator with no automatic invocation, invisible at runtime until caller invokes `assemble()`) with `default_budget_tokens=8000`, three ratio fields validated by `@field_validator` (each in [0.0, 1.0]) + `@model_validator(mode='after')` (sum 1.0 ±0.01 tolerance). Wired onto `SystemConfig.diagnostic_context` via `Field(default_factory=DiagnosticContextConfig)` immediately after `causal_reasoning` field. Sync `_wire_diagnostic_context(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirrors `_wire_chain_optimizer`/`_wire_causal_reasoner` shape exactly; invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_causal_reasoner`. Public attribute `runtime.diagnostic_context_service` (Wave 5 convention #1). New `src/probos/routers/diagnostic_context.py` with `GET /api/diagnostic-context?query=&budget=&agent_id=&since=` (budget hard-capped at 32000 via `min(max(budget, 1), 32000)`; HTTPException 503 when `runtime.diagnostic_context_service is None`; `since: float | None` Unix-timestamp → `datetime.fromtimestamp(tz=timezone.utc)` before passing to service). Registered in `api.py` router-tuple alphabetically appended to import + for-loop tuples (twin-block SEARCH/REPLACE bundled into single combined block per Wave 31 pattern). 8 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad661_diagnostic_context.py` (over 7 floor by 1): bundle frozen mutation raises + `to_dict` deep-copy mutability isolation; keyword extractor min-len drop + matcher empty-kw + None-haystack + estimator boundary + `CHARS_PER_TOKEN` constant; chain_trace keyword filter (cpu sub_task_type matches c1/c3, REPORT excluded) + budget clip (40% of 2000 = 800 chain budget < c3's 6000-char error_truncated → `truncated=True`); procedure exemplar resolution via `get_by_ids` (single call with full id list, episodes deduped flat list ep_a + ep_b); budget truncation flag (50 rows × ~200 tokens >> 400-token chain budget → `truncated=True` + `len < 50`); episode dedup across multiple procedures (ep_shared appears once, ep_a + ep_b each appear once); collector failure log-and-degrade (RuntimeError from `get_recent_chain_traces` → empty sections + caplog WARNING contains `AD-661`); API endpoint 503 when service None + 200 happy path with TestClient + dependency_overrides + asserted `assemble` kwargs (query/budget_tokens/agent_id). Full gate: 10950 passed, 15 skipped, 1 known xdist-worker flake (`test_episode_clustering::test_last_clusters_property` — passes serial in 7.68s; same xdist concurrent-fixture-boot pattern documented in Waves 23/27/30/31/32 baselines). Test-count delta: +8 vs Wave 32 baseline 10942 (exact target hit). Baseline collected count drift: 10958 → 10966 (+8 exact). No hard-stops triggered: no automatic invocation (deferred AD-661b/d), no semantic search (deferred AD-661d), no LLM summary (deferred AD-661e), no AD-434 integration (deferred AD-661b), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed all 11 prompt-asserted symbols live at HEAD `4787c9d`: `CognitiveJournal.get_recent_chain_traces`, `Procedure.trace_exemplars`, `EpisodicMemory.get_by_ids`, `ProcedureStore.list_active`/`get`, `CHARS_PER_TOKEN` precedent, `_wire_chain_optimizer` sibling shape, api.py router-tuple twin-block, `runtime.records_store` referenced not consumed). Closes GH issue #320.

AD-660 v1 CLOSED. Agent Causal Reasoning Framework — TEMPLATE + JOURNAL + INTEGRATION POINT (GH issue #319, Wave 32). Lee et al. Meta-Harness proposer four-step protocol (arXiv:2603.28052) operationalized as a structured metacognitive artifact agents fill when an unexpected outcome triggers analysis. v1 ships **template + storage + one opt-in integration point** ONLY — there is NO causal-inference engine, NO automatic invocation, NO hypothesis ranking, NO diagnostic-action execution, NO emergence-event wiring. The LLM fills the four steps (`what_changed`, `confounded_variables`, `testable_hypotheses`, `diagnostic_actions` + self-reported `confidence`); ProbOS persists. New `src/probos/cognitive/causal_reasoning.py` with `CausalReasoningTemplate` **frozen** dataclass (10 fields: `template_id`, `agent_id`, `triggered_at`, `trigger_summary`, four list[str] step fields capped at 8 items × 500 chars each, `confidence` 0.0–1.0, optional `source_event_ref` correlation token; `to_dict()` projection ISO-formats datetime), `_coerce_list`/`_coerce_confidence`/`_empty_template` helpers, `CausalReasoner` service (`__init__(runtime, *, max_tokens=700, tier="standard")` mirrors AD-659 sibling shape; two never-raises async methods: `analyze(*, trigger, agent_id, context=None, source_event_ref=None) -> CausalReasoningTemplate` calls `runtime.llm_client.complete(LLMRequest)` with structured system prompt + JSON-only user prompt, parses via `extract_json`, degrades to empty-template on missing client / LLM exception / parse failure; `analyze_concern(concern_data) -> CausalReasoningTemplate | None` convenience wrapper that returns None on missing `agent_id`, otherwise builds an AD-504-shaped trigger summary from `agent_callsign`/`zone`/`similarity_ratio`/`velocity_ratio` and source_event_ref `self_monitoring_concern:{agent_id}`). Journal extended (mirroring AD-658 chain_traces pattern exactly): added `import json`, new `_SCHEMA_CAUSAL_TEMPLATES` table (10-column, 2 indexes on `triggered_at`+`agent_id`) provisioned via second `executescript` in `start()`, new `record_causal_template(template)` (INSERT OR IGNORE on PK `template_id`, JSON-serializes 4 list fields, datetime → epoch float via `.timestamp()`, fire-and-forget never raises), new `get_recent_causal_templates(*, limit=50, agent_id=None, since=None)` (most-recent-first ORDER BY triggered_at DESC, optional filters, JSON-decodes list fields back to list[str] on read, returns [] on no-db / query failure), `prune(...)` extended with both age-based and row-cap branches for `causal_templates`. New `CausalReasoningConfig(enabled=False, max_tokens=700, tier="standard")` Pydantic model (default-False per Wave 10 transitional-flag lesson) wired onto `SystemConfig.causal_reasoning` immediately after `chain_optimizer` field. Sync `_wire_causal_reasoner(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirrors `_wire_chain_optimizer` shape exactly; invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_chain_optimizer`. Public attribute `runtime.causal_reasoner` (Wave 5 convention #1). Integration POINT: ONE guarded block in `counselor._on_self_monitoring_concern` after `_save_profile_and_assessment` — uses existing `self._runtime` (CognitiveAgent property) to read `runtime.causal_reasoner` and existing `self._cognitive_journal` property (cognitive_agent.py:265 — proxies to `runtime.cognitive_journal`); fire-and-forget try/except → `logger.debug` with "AD-660" tag (must NOT raise into existing counselor flow). With default config, `runtime.causal_reasoner` is not constructed and the hook is a no-op (convention #14 — invisible at runtime on first commit). **What this AD does NOT change**: no causal-inference engine; no automatic invocation across other concern paths; no AD-557 emergence-event wiring (deferred AD-660b); no hypothesis ranking / no scoring / no de-dup (deferred AD-660b); no diagnostic-action execution (deferred AD-660c); no new EventType; no `/api/causal-templates` router (deferred AD-660b); no HXI surface; no ChromaDB / no Git knowledge store; no retroactive backfill; no chain-trace co-correlation; no counselor logic changes (hook runs strictly as side-channel after profile save); no ChainOptimizer integration (deferred AD-660d). **Builder drift-fix**: prompt's hook used `getattr(self._runtime, "causal_reasoner", None)` and `getattr(self._runtime, "cognitive_journal", None)` but Counselor is a CognitiveAgent subclass that already has `_cognitive_journal` as a `@property` (cognitive_agent.py:265) — direct field shadowing failed `AttributeError: property has no setter` across 235 tests. Reverted counselor field/initialize-kwarg additions; hook now uses `self._runtime` (existing CognitiveAgent attribute) for reasoner lookup and `self._cognitive_journal` (existing property) for journal access — no counselor surface change beyond the single hook insertion. 8 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad660_causal_reasoning.py` (matches prompt target — exceeds 7 floor by 1): frozen-dataclass mutation raises + `to_dict` ISO datetime round-trip; happy-path with synthetic LLM JSON (5 fields populated, confidence=0.6, complete awaited once); degraded-path with non-JSON LLM content (empty lists + confidence=0.0); journal record + retrieve round-trip with real `CognitiveJournal` against tmp_path (10 fields verified); journal `agent_id` filter (3 inserts × 2 agents → only 2 rows for "a1"); `analyze_concern` returns None on missing agent_id without invoking LLM; wirer skips when `enabled=False` (no `runtime.causal_reasoner` attribute set); wirer creates `runtime.causal_reasoner` instance when enabled + smoke `analyze_concern` returns degraded template on `{}` LLM response. Full gate: 10942 passed, 15 skipped, 1 known-flake (knowledge_store auto_commit, pre-existing baseline noise). Test-count delta: +9 vs Wave 31 baseline 10933 (+8 new + 1 dreaming flake flipped to pass — net +8 owned by AD-660). No hard-stops triggered: no inference engine (deferred AD-660b/c), no automatic invocation (deferred AD-660b), no action execution (deferred AD-660c), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at build time confirmed `SELF_MONITORING_CONCERN`/`_on_self_monitoring_concern`/`_SCHEMA_CHAIN_TRACES`/`record_chain_trace`/`_wire_chain_optimizer`/`ChainOptimizerConfig`/`extract_json`/`LLMRequest`/`runtime.cognitive_journal`/`runtime.llm_client`/`CognitiveAgent._cognitive_journal` property — all 11 prompt-asserted symbols verified live at HEAD; phantom flagged late: prompt assumed counselor had `self._runtime` field directly but verified at build that CognitiveAgent base provides it; the pre-check spec did not surface the existing `_cognitive_journal` property collision until xdist gate caught it). Closes GH issue #319.

AD-659 v1 CLOSED. Cognitive Chain Self-Optimization Loop — ANALYSIS ONLY (GH issue #318, Wave 31). Meta-Harness outer-loop research (Lee et al., arXiv:2603.28052) — propose, evaluate, store, iterate. v1 ships **propose** + Captain approval surface ONLY; **evaluate** (A/B framework) and **iterate** (apply) are explicitly deferred. No automated prompt rewriting, no automatic parameter adjustment, no live mutation of ChainTuningConfig. New `src/probos/cognitive/chain_optimizer.py` with `OptimizationProposal` mutable dataclass (mutable to support post-construction `decide()` setting `decision`/`decided_at`/`decided_by`; `to_dict()` projection), `ChainOptimizer` service (ctor `(runtime, *, analysis_window=100, latency_p95_ms_floor=10000.0, success_rate_floor=0.7, error_rate_ceiling=0.3, min_samples_per_group=20, emit_event=None)` mirrors AD-508 sibling shape), and three pure detector functions: `detect_latency_p95_regression` (group by `(step_name, tier)`; p95 over floor proposes tier shift `standard`→`fast` or `deep`→`standard`; medium risk), `detect_success_rate_floor_breach` (group by `(sub_task_type, chain_trust_band)`; below-floor proposes nudging `chain_tuning.low_trust_ceiling` or `high_trust_floor` by +0.05; mid/unknown bands skipped — no single-knob fix; medium risk), `detect_high_error_rate_by_chain_source` (group by `chain_source`; above-ceiling proposes `chain_source.review[X]` observation-only flag; low risk). Public service API: `analyze(window=None)` reads `runtime.cognitive_journal.get_recent_chain_traces(limit=n)` (AD-658 surface), runs all three detectors, appends to `pending_proposals` (in-memory; no dedup, no persistence — deferred AD-659b); `list_pending()` returns undecided slice; `decide(proposal_id, decision, *, actor="captain")` records approve/reject (raises ValueError on bad decision); `apply_proposal(...)` raises `NotImplementedError("v1 analysis-only; apply deferred to AD-659b")` — v1 hard limit. Detectors are pure (`list[dict]` + kwarg thresholds → `list[OptimizationProposal]`, no I/O, no runtime access). Adjustable-vs-derived modulation surface honored: v1 proposes only against `ChainTuningConfig.low_trust_ceiling`/`high_trust_floor` (config.py:331–332, tunable); `_chain_trust_band`/`_trust_score`/`_communication_context`/`_boot_camp_active` are observation-derived (cognitive_agent.py:2061/2076–2085/2066–2068) and explicitly out of scope for v1. New `ChainOptimizerConfig(enabled=False)` Pydantic model wired onto `SystemConfig.chain_optimizer` (default-False per Wave 10 transitional-flag lesson; Captain opts in via YAML once AD-658 baseline data accumulated). Sync `_wire_chain_optimizer(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirrors `_wire_duty_scope_provider` shape exactly; invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_duty_scope_provider`. Public attribute `runtime.chain_optimizer` (Wave 5 convention #1). New `src/probos/routers/chain_optimizer.py` with `GET /api/chain-optimizer/proposals?include_decided=False` (returns `{proposals, enabled}`; `enabled=False` when wirer not active) and `POST /api/chain-optimizer/proposals/{id}/decide` body `{decision: "approve"|"reject", actor="captain"}` returning `{status, applied: False, proposal}` — explicit `"applied": False` v1 limitation surfaced in API response; mirrors `routers/build.py:150–177` build-queue approval REST shape; HTTPException 503 when optimizer disabled, 400 on bad decision, 404 on missing id. Registered in `api.py` router-tuple alphabetically between `chain_traces` and `counselor` (twin-block import + for-loop tuple combined into single SEARCH/REPLACE). Privacy: no chain-trace contents in event payload (v1 emits no events; emission deferred to AD-659b). **What this AD does NOT change**: no `apply_proposal()` implementation (deferred AD-659b); no proposal persistence (in-memory; restart clears queue); no A/B framework execution; no automatic prompt rewriting; no Counselor regression watchdog (deferred AD-659c); no new EventType; no periodic background analyze loop (Captain triggers manually); no proposal de-duplication (re-running `analyze()` over same window appends fresh batch; AD-659b dedups on `(detector_name, target_parameter)`); no rollback (no apply path to roll back); no AD-658 schema changes; no `ChainTuningConfig` mutation (v1 PROPOSES adjustments; mutating live config is the apply path AD-659b). 8 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad659_chain_self_optimization.py` (matches prompt target — exceeds 7 floor by 1): 5 detector-shape (3 happy-path + 1 below-min-samples branch + 1 mid-band no-proposal branch); 1 service aggregation+decide (all three detectors triggered via 75-trace fixture, list_pending undecided slice, decide flips fields); 1 apply_proposal NotImplementedError matches "AD-659b"; 1 API end-to-end via FastAPI TestClient with stub runtime (GET pending, POST decide returns `applied: False`, GET pending now empty, GET include_decided still returns it, POST garbage decision → 400, POST missing id → 404). Full gate: 10935 passed, 15 skipped (delta +8 vs Wave 30 baseline 10927 — exact target hit). No hard-stops triggered: no apply path (deferred AD-659b), no A/B execution (deferred AD-659b), no Counselor wiring (deferred AD-659c), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time confirmed `ChainExecutionTrace`/`get_recent_chain_traces`/`ChainTuningConfig.low_trust_ceiling+high_trust_floor`/`_wire_duty_scope_provider`/`runtime.cognitive_journal`/`runtime.emit_event`/build-queue approval shape — all 7 prompt-asserted symbols verified live at HEAD `9b3abc7`). Drift-fix during build: prompt's first detector test fixture had p95 idx=22 landing in low-band slice (sorted index 22 of 25 with 23 ones+2 twelves → 1000ms not 12000ms); fixed by uniform 12000ms across all 25 traces in both Test #1 and the service-aggregation latency block. Closes GH issue #318.

AD-683 v1 CLOSED. Ship State Snapshot for Cold-Start Onboarding (GH issue #313, Wave 29). Meta-Harness research (Lee et al., arXiv:2603.28052) showed environment bootstrapping eliminates 2-4 exploratory turns in agentic coding; ProbOS equivalent injects one structured snapshot into the cold-start agent's first DM user-message. New `src/probos/onboarding/` package with `ship_state_snapshot.py`: three frozen dataclasses (`DepartmentSummary`, `WardRoomTopicSummary`, `ShipStateSnapshot` — `captured_at` first non-defaulted field per standing convention, `to_dict()`/`render_text()` projections) and `ShipStateSnapshotBuilder` service (ctor `(runtime, *, emit_event=None)` mirrors AD-508 `DutyScopeProvider` shape; single public `async build() -> ShipStateSnapshot`). Each per-source collector wrapped in try/except → `logger.warning` + degraded default (tier-2 log-and-degrade); `build()` never raises. Hard caps inline (NOT exposed): `_MAX_OPEN_WORK_ITEMS=5`, `_MAX_TOPIC_CHANNELS=3`, `_MAX_THREAD_TITLES_PER_CHANNEL=3`, `_TITLE_TRUNCATE_CHARS=80`. Collectors: `_collect_vessel` reads `ontology.get_vessel_identity()`/`get_vessel_state()`; `_collect_departments` reads `ontology.get_departments()` + derives crew counts via `get_crew_agent_types()`/`get_assignment_for_agent()`/`get_post_for_agent()`; `_collect_work_items` calls `work_item_store.list_work_items(status="open", limit=5)`; `_collect_ward_room_topics` calls `ward_room.list_channels()` + per-channel `list_threads(channel.id, limit=3, sort="recent")`. New `ShipStateSnapshotConfig(enabled=True)` Pydantic model wired onto `SystemConfig.ship_state_snapshot` via `Field(default_factory=ShipStateSnapshotConfig)`. Sync `_wire_ship_state_snapshot(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirrors `_wire_boot_camp_tracker` shape; invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_boot_camp_tracker`. Public attribute `runtime.ship_state_snapshot` IS the builder service (no third-state cache; mirrors AD-508 naming). `BootCampCoordinator.__init__` extended with optional kwarg `ship_state_builder: Any | None = None` (default preserves backward-compat for all existing AD-638 tests/callers; verified by `test_boot_camp_coordinator_backward_compat_and_wirer`); new public attribute `coord.ship_state_snapshot: ShipStateSnapshot | None = None`. At end of `BootCampCoordinator.activate(...)` (after existing `BOOT_CAMP_ACTIVATED` emit) — if `_ship_state_builder is not None`, await `build()` and assign to `self.ship_state_snapshot`; wrapped in try/except → log-and-degrade. `runtime.py:1580` BootCampCoordinator construction passes `ship_state_builder=getattr(self, "ship_state_snapshot", None)` (defends against startup-ordering inversion since wirer runs in `finalize_startup` after `start()`-time construction; `getattr(...None)` makes either ordering correct). DM-path render in `cognitive_agent._build_user_message` — new block at top of `parts` list (BEFORE temporal-awareness) gated on `observation.get("_boot_camp_active") and self._runtime is not None`; reads `runtime.boot_camp.ship_state_snapshot`; calls `render_text()` (debug-log + skip on render failure — tier-1 swallow since user message must still be sent); prepends `--- Ship State Snapshot ---` / `<text>` / `---` / blank line. WR-path and chain-path injection explicitly out-of-scope (AD-683e). 1 new EventType `SHIP_STATE_SNAPSHOT_CAPTURED` (verified collision-free against events.py post-Wave-28). Event payload contains COUNTS only (`captured_at`, `alert_condition`, `work_item_count`, `dept_count`, `topic_count`) — privacy-conservative per AD-530 / AD-478 pattern; directly tested at `test_builder_happy_path_full_data` ("no titles, no department names, no thread bodies in payload"). v1 ships builder + capture-at-activation + DM-path render only per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral. **Deferred**: AD-683b (per-agent personalization), AD-683c (snapshot deltas/refreshes — forcing function: AD-683 ships and Captain reviews captured snapshots; if staleness observed, externalize refresh policy), AD-683d (federation sync), AD-683e (chain-path ANALYZE/EVALUATE/COMPOSE injection), AD-683f (Ward Room post-path injection), AD-683g (persistence to ChromaDB/disk/journal). **No `BOOT_CAMP_ACTIVATED` payload changes**, no AD-509 phase tracker integration, no AD-477 PlanOfDayService refactor (same data sources, different consumer; no shared aggregator extraction in v1). 8 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad683_ship_state_snapshot.py` (matches prompt target — exceeds 6-floor by 2): EventType literal value; frozen dataclass + field-order (`captured_at` first) + `to_dict` round-trip with nested DepartmentSummary/WardRoomTopicSummary; `render_text` includes vessel/alert/uptime int-cast/crew/work-item title/topic line; builder happy-path full data with `_make_runtime_full()` SimpleNamespace fixture (Enterprise/YELLOW/42.0/7 + 2 departments with crew counts derived from assignments + 3 work items + 2 channels × 2 threads) plus emit_event privacy assertion (no titles, no dept names, no thread bodies); collectors-missing degraded path (no ontology/work_item_store/ward_room → all defaults, no exception); collectors-raise degraded path (`RuntimeError("boom")` from each collector → all defaults + caplog WARNING contains "AD-683"); capture-in-activate (ordering: `_active is True` BEFORE snapshot captured, `coord.ship_state_snapshot` populated `ShipStateSnapshot` instance with vessel_name "Enterprise"); combined backward-compat-no-kwarg + wirer enabled/disabled (3 sub-assertions). Full gate: 10920 passed, 15 skipped (delta +8 vs Wave 28 baseline 10912 — exact target hit). No hard-stops triggered: no per-agent personalization (deferred AD-683b), no deltas/refreshes (deferred AD-683c), no federation sync (deferred AD-683d), no chain-path injection (deferred AD-683e), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at build time confirmed `BootCampCoordinator` ctor + `runtime.boot_camp` attribute + ontology API methods + `work_item_store.list_work_items` signature + `ward_room.list_channels`/`list_threads` signatures + `_boot_camp_active` observation flag set sites + `_build_user_message` DM branch entry + `_wire_boot_camp_tracker` sibling shape + `BOOT_CAMP_ACTIVATED` event entry — all 9 prompt-asserted symbols verified live at HEAD `b76b8a8`). `SHIP_STATE_SNAPSHOT_CAPTURED` newly registered. Closes GH issue #313.

AD-658 v1 CLOSED. Cognitive Chain Harness Metrics (GH issue #317). Measurement substrate for AD-659 chain optimization (Meta-Harness research, Lee et al., arXiv:2603.28052 — "the cognitive chain IS a harness; you can't optimize what you can't measure"). New `src/probos/cognitive/chain_trace.py` with `ChainExecutionTrace` frozen dataclass (24 fields: chain/step identity, caller identity, wall-clock+execution, context composition breakdown, modulation snapshot from AD-649/AD-639/AD-638) + `to_dict()` projection. New `_SCHEMA_CHAIN_TRACES` table on `CognitiveJournal` provisioned via `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` + 3 indexes (started_at, agent_id, chain_id) at `start()`. Two new fire-and-forget never-raises methods on `CognitiveJournal`: `record_chain_trace(trace)` (INSERT OR IGNORE on `(chain_id, step_index)` write-once PK; mirrors existing `record(...)` shape) and `get_recent_chain_traces(*, limit=50, agent_id=None, since=None) -> list[dict]` (mirrors `get_reasoning_chain` shape). `prune(...)` extended to also age-based DELETE on `chain_traces` (`started_at < cutoff`) and row-count-cap with rowid ordering (PK is composite `(chain_id, step_index)`, not single `id`). New `chain_source: str = ""` kwarg on `SubTaskExecutor._execute_single_step` plumbed from `chain.source` through both `_execute_steps` call sites (single-step branch + parallel-wave list comp). New wall-clock `step_started_at = time.time()` captured next to existing monotonic `step_start`. Per-step trace-emission block inserted between existing `journal.record(...)` block and the abort-on-required-failure raise; wrapped in try/except (telemetry must never break the chain — Convention #15 swallow tier); `hasattr(journal, "record_chain_trace")` guard for non-`CognitiveJournal` mocks. Modulation params read from full `context` (the observation), NOT `step_context` (the filtered view) — they are properties of the run, not the step's prompt-context budget. New `src/probos/routers/chain_traces.py` with `GET /api/chain-traces` (limit hard-capped at 500, optional `agent_id`/`since` filters); registered in `api.py` router-tuple alphabetically between `chat` and `counselor`. **What this AD does NOT change**: no output quality signals beyond `success` (deferred AD-659); no input/output token split (defer AD-658a); no handler signature changes; no new EventType (DB-only emission); no WebSocket fan-out; no retroactive backfill; no sampling. **AD-652 dependency satisfied as-is** (design principle adopted in DECISIONS.md:1914; realised across AD-649/AD-639/AD-638 — no wiring changes). 8 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad658_chain_harness_metrics.py` (matches prompt target): dataclass defaults + `to_dict` round-trip; frozen-mutation raises FrozenInstanceError; real-`CognitiveJournal`-against-tmp-path round-trip with all 24 fields; filter+ordering (3 traces, agent_id filter, since filter, limit cap); no-db short-circuit returns None/[]; executor emits trace per step with full modulation snapshot (`MagicMock` journal); context-composition breakdown records ANALYZE-with-filter (declared=2, passed=3 with `_internal` underscore-passthrough, filter_applied=True) and QUERY-without-filter (passed=4, filter_applied=False); trace emission failure (`AsyncMock(side_effect=Exception)`) does not break chain (results returned, no raise). Full gate: 10912 passed, 15 skipped (delta +8 vs Wave 27 baseline 10904 — exact target hit). No hard-stops triggered: no quality signals (deferred AD-659), no token split (deferred AD-658a), no live streaming (deferred AD-658a), no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation (verify-first pre-check at draft time documented all symbol introductions). Closes GH issue #317.

 Dream Consolidation Trace Preservation (GH issue #316). Preserves the 2-3 most diagnostically rich source episodes per consolidated `Procedure` and surfaces them in proactive context when a matching dream pattern is recalled, grounding abstractions in concrete diagnostic detail (Meta-Harness research: full traces scored 50.0% median vs 34.9% for summaries — Lee et al., arXiv:2603.28052). New `Procedure.trace_exemplars: list[str]` field with `to_dict`/`from_dict` round-trip and backward-compat default `[]` for legacy serialized procedures. New `DreamingConfig.trace_exemplars_per_procedure: int = 3` (0 disables). Step 7 in `dreaming.py` ranks `matched_episodes` by `(importance DESC, timestamp DESC)` and slices `[:n_exemplars]` — populated unconditionally inside the existing `if procedure:` branch so chain, compound, and standard extractions all benefit; runs BEFORE the existing dedup gate so the field is set on every procedure that reaches `procedure_store.save()`. New `EpisodicMemory.get_by_ids(episode_ids: list[str]) -> list[Episode]` read primitive — wraps ChromaDB `_collection.get(ids=..., include=["metadatas","documents"])`, reuses the existing `_metadata_to_episode` static, returns episodes in input order, silently omits missing IDs (graceful degradation when AD-593 activation pruning has evicted an exemplar). New consumer block in `proactive.py:_gather_context` after the existing `recent_memories` block — uses sibling `try` for failure isolation; reads `runtime.procedure_store` (public property at `runtime.py:961`) and `runtime.episodic_memory` (public attribute at `runtime.py:380`); calls `find_matching(query, n_results=1, exclude_negative=True)` with the same `f"{agent.agent_type} recent duty observations"` query shape as the recall_weighted path; gates on `score >= 0.5`; emits `context["recalled_procedure_exemplars"] = {"procedure_name", "procedure_id", "exemplars": [{"input", "reflection", "importance", "age"}]}` with 300-char per-field truncation and `[trimmed]` marker. **No new EventType, no new module, no `runtime._private` access, no schema migration, no backfill, no mutation of `Procedure.provenance`** (stays as the full superset; `trace_exemplars` is a curated subset). Demeter clean. Hard-coded thresholds (top-1 procedure match, ≤3 exemplars, score floor 0.5, 300-char per-field cap) — externalized only if AD-657a integration data justifies tuning. **What v1 ships of the roadmap entry**: structure + producer + 1 consumer (proactive context). **Deferred**: re-ranking after extraction (set ONCE; Procedures evolved via FIX/DERIVED keep parent's exemplars unless evolution path explicitly sets new ones), backfill of existing procedures (forward-only), `cognitive_agent._check_procedural_memory()` integration (replay-first dispatch path stays untouched), score-floor / context-budget config externalization, anchor-aware exemplar diversification (e.g., diversify by department). One drift-fix in `tests/test_procedure_extraction.py::test_procedure_to_dict_includes_all_fields` to add `trace_exemplars` to the expected_keys set. 10 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad657_trace_exemplars.py` (2 over the prompt's 7-floor — added `_episodic_get_by_ids_no_collection_returns_empty` for the `_collection is None` short-circuit and `_gather_context_omits_exemplars_when_score_below_floor` for the score-floor branch): Procedure schema default + round-trip + backward-compat (3); Step 7 ranking by `(importance, timestamp)` DESC with 9/9/5/3/1 + 40/20/30/10/50 fixture verifying tie-break order (1); cap at config N=2 (1); disabled when N=0 (1); `get_by_ids` real-ChromaDB integration with input-order preservation + missing-ID drop + empty-input short-circuit (1); `get_by_ids` no-collection short-circuit (1); consumer happy path with 300-char truncation marker assertion + 408-char input → 308+len(marker) char output (1); consumer omits when all exemplar episodes pruned (1); consumer omits when match score below 0.5 floor (1). Full gate: 10903 passed, 15 skipped + 1 environmental flake (`test_knowledge_store::test_auto_commit_after_debounce` — same xdist git-debounce timing flake documented under multiple Wave baselines; passes serially in 0.70s; unrelated to this AD). Test delta vs Wave 26 baseline (10894 passed): +9 (10 new tests in test_ad657_trace_exemplars.py − 1 test in test_procedure_extraction.py was already counted but its key-set changed). **Anti-pattern caught at code-review time**: prompt referenced `ProactiveThoughtGenerator(runtime=...)` constructor; live class is `ProactiveCognitiveLoop` with `set_runtime()` setter — folded to live API in test fixtures; not a phantom-API in implementation (consumer block uses `getattr(rt, ...)` pattern matching live runtime shape), only a phantom in test scaffolding mental model. No hard-stops triggered. Closes GH issue #316.
 New `PostBudgetTelemetry` in `ward_room_pipeline.py` with per-agent / per-thread / overall invocation + exhaustion counters, `exhaustion_rate(agent_type=, thread_id=)` accessor, bounded recent-suppressions ring buffer, and one-shot per-agent WARN when rate exceeds configurable threshold (default 0.5) and min-samples gate (default 10). New `PostBudgetTelemetryConfig` wired onto `SystemConfig.post_budget_telemetry`. Public attribute `runtime.post_budget_telemetry`. Constructor injection into `WardRoomPostPipeline` via new `post_budget_telemetry=` kwarg; default `None` preserves all existing pipeline call sites. BF-237 `event_log` row-level audit trail preserved unchanged; observational only — never mutates budget, never blocks posts. 8 focused tests pass at `tests/test_bf238_post_budget_telemetry.py`. Full gate: 10894 passed, 15 skipped (delta +8 vs Wave 25 baseline 10886 — exact target hit). No hard-stops triggered. Closes GH issue #341.
AD-509 v1 CLOSED. Onboarding Curriculum Pipeline — Boot Camp Phase Tracker (1 of 5). New `src/probos/crew_development/boot_camp.py` (alongside AD-507 `curriculum.py`) with `BootCampPhase` str-Enum (5 phases + COMPLETED sentinel: ORIENTATION, CORE_KNOWLEDGE, A_SCHOOL, CALIBRATION, INTEGRATION, COMPLETED), module-level immutable `_PHASE_ORDER` tuple defining canonical progression, `AgentBootCampRecord` dataclass (`agent_id`, `current_phase` defaulting ORIENTATION, `started_at: float`, `phase_history: list[tuple[str, float]]`, `to_dict()` projection), and `BootCampPhaseTracker` (in-memory `dict[str, AgentBootCampRecord]`). Public API: `get_or_create(agent_id)` idempotent + seeds initial ORIENTATION history entry on creation; `advance_phase(agent_id) -> BootCampPhase` returns next phase, no-op once COMPLETED, appends `(phase_value, time.time())` to `phase_history`, emits `BOOT_CAMP_PHASE_ADVANCED`; `get_record(agent_id)` returns record or None; `all_records()` returns stable tuple; `is_completed(agent_id)` returns True only at COMPLETED phase. 1 new EventType `BOOT_CAMP_PHASE_ADVANCED` (verified collision-free against events.py post-Wave-24). Event payload `{agent_id, previous_phase, current_phase}` (string values from enum). **Naming-collision resolution** — Wave 25 prompt drafted `BootCampConfig` + `SystemConfig.boot_camp` but `BootCampConfig` (AD-638 cold-start boot camp) + `SystemConfig.boot_camp` already exist; disambiguated to `BootCampPhaseConfig` + `SystemConfig.boot_camp_phase` to preserve AD-638 surface. Public runtime attribute `runtime.boot_camp_tracker` is collision-free per prompt pre-check (verified 0 hits prior to build). New `BootCampPhaseConfig(enabled=True)` Pydantic model wired onto `SystemConfig.boot_camp_phase: BootCampPhaseConfig = Field(default_factory=BootCampPhaseConfig)`. Sync `_wire_boot_camp_tracker(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirrors AD-507/AD-525/AD-530/AD-511 sync-wiring shape (no awaits in body); invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_curriculum_registry`. `emit_event` is a public field on the tracker (mirrors AD-507/AD-456/AD-530/AD-511 sibling pattern); `logger.warning` on emit failure (log-and-degrade tier). Wave 25 SKIPPED FORMAL REVIEW (low-risk in-memory tracker AD; sibling pattern proven via AD-507 + AD-525 + AD-511 + AD-487 — same Pydantic-config + sync-wirer + public-runtime-attr + emit_event-public-field shape; pre-check returned 1 documented FP only). v1 ships 1 of 5 AD-509 capabilities per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral: (1) `BootCampPhaseTracker` (in-memory observational only). Deferred: AD-509b (A-School per-department curriculum — depends on AD-507 partial-shipped; could be next wave); AD-509c (Graduated stimuli + cognitive load monitoring); AD-509d (Completion criteria gating — replaces time-based activation); AD-509e (Trait-adaptive pacing — depends on AD-486). **OBSERVATIONAL** — tracker just records caller-driven advancements; never gates phase transitions, never adapts pacing, never triggers Holodeck scenarios, never integrates with AD-486 Birth Chamber. **No consumers in v1** — AD-486 Holodeck, AD-507 Curriculum Registry, AD-477 Qualification Gates, proactive cognitive loop are read-only future consumers; none wired in v1. 15 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad509_boot_camp.py` (matches prompt's ~15 target): Section 0 EventType existence with literal value assertion; Section 4 Pydantic config defaults; Section 2 enum-shape (5 phases + COMPLETED, total 6) and `AgentBootCampRecord` initial state; Section 3 `get_or_create` idempotency + seed-history-entry, `advance_phase` progresses through canonical order, `advance_phase` stops at COMPLETED with phase_history fixed at 6 entries, `advance_phase` emits event with full payload, `advance_phase` records phase_history correctly, `get_record` hit/miss, `all_records` returns tuple, `is_completed` returns True only at COMPLETED phase; Section 5 wiring tests for enabled/disabled config. Full gate: 10885 passed, 15 skipped + 1 pre-existing flake (`test_resolve_refires_after_clean_period` — re-ran in isolation: PASSED in 7.41s; xdist-noise pattern; unrelated to this AD). Test delta vs Wave 24 baseline (10870 passed): +15 (exact match). No hard-stops triggered: no A-School curriculum (deferred AD-509b), no graduated stimuli (deferred AD-509c), no completion-criteria gating (deferred AD-509d), no trait-adaptive pacing (deferred AD-509e), no Holodeck integration, no scope creep, no phantoms in implementation. Naming-collision resolution documented under DECISIONS.md Era V; not an architectural change. `BOOT_CAMP_PHASE_ADVANCED` newly registered. Closes GH issue #91.
AD-507 v1 CLOSED. Crew Development Framework -- Core Knowledge Curriculum Registry (1 of 4). New src/probos/crew_development/ package with curriculum.py containing CurriculumModule frozen dataclass (module_id/	itle/category/summary/learning_objectives: tuple[str, ...]/delivery_phase) and CoreKnowledgeCurriculumRegistry (read-only catalog; public API: list_modules() -> tuple[CurriculumModule, ...], get_module(module_id) -> CurriculumModule | None, list_by_category(category), list_by_phase(phase), 
egister_module(module) runtime-only idempotent overwrite-by-id; not persisted in v1). egister_module is runtime-only) wired onto SystemConfig.crew_development: CrewDevelopmentConfig = Field(default_factory=CrewDevelopmentConfig). untime.curriculum_registry. mit_event is a public field on the registry (mirrors AD-456/AD-530/AD-511 sibling pattern); logger.warning on emit failure (log-and-degrade tier).
