AD-508 v1 + AD-478 v1 (Combo E) CLOSED. Scoped Cognition (Duty Scope helper) + Meta-Learning (Workspace Ontology read-only register) — observational cognitive helpers shipped together per Wave 8 Combo A + Wave 13 Combo C precedent. **AD-508 v1 (Duty Scope)**: New `src/probos/cognitive/scoped_cognition.py` with `DutyScopeProvider` (async `snapshot(agent_id) -> DutyScopeSnapshot` reads `runtime.work_item_store.list_work_items(status="open", assigned_to=agent_id, limit=5)` — verified live signature at workforce.py:1066) and `DutyScopeSnapshot` frozen dataclass (`agent_id`/`open_work_item_count`/`work_item_titles` up to 5 titles from `WorkItem.title`/`captured_at`). Empty snapshot when `agent_id` falsy or `work_item_store` missing; `list_work_items` failures logged at debug and produce empty snapshot (log-and-degrade). Constructor `__init__(runtime, *, emit_event=None)` mirrors AD-530 `ClassificationGate` sibling pattern (pass-1 Rec1 folded). Emits `DUTY_SCOPE_QUERIED` per snapshot with `{agent_id, open_count}` payload. **AD-478 v1 (Workspace Ontology)**: New `src/probos/cognitive/workspace_ontology.py` with `WorkspaceOntologyRegistry` (in-memory frequency-bounded term registry; default `max_terms=1000`). Public API: `add_term(term, frequency=1)`, `top_terms(k=20)`, `get_frequency(term)`, `term_count()`. Eviction policy: when `len(_terms)` exceeds `max_terms`, the lowest-frequency term is dropped (insertion-order tie-break per dict-preserves-insertion + stable `min`; pass-1 Nit2 folded — docstring explicit). Empty `term` is a no-op. `top_terms` returns `()` for `k <= 0` (pass-1 Nit3 folded — explicit boundary test `test_top_terms_k_zero_returns_empty`). Emits `WORKSPACE_TERM_REGISTERED` only on first insertion (not on increments) to bound event volume. **Privacy invariant** (directly tested at `test_add_term_emits_event_only_on_new_term_with_term_length`): `WORKSPACE_TERM_REGISTERED` payload includes `term_length` (NOT the term itself), matching AD-530/AD-511 pattern; test asserts no `term` key appears in payload. 2 new EventTypes (`DUTY_SCOPE_QUERIED`, `WORKSPACE_TERM_REGISTERED`; verified collision-free against events.py post-Wave-22). 2 new Pydantic configs: `ScopedCognitionConfig(enabled=True)` and `WorkspaceOntologyConfig(enabled=True, max_terms=1000)` wired onto `SystemConfig.scoped_cognition` and `SystemConfig.workspace_ontology` via `Field(default_factory=...)`. Sync `_wire_duty_scope_provider(*, runtime, config) -> bool` and `_wire_workspace_ontology(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirror AD-525/AD-530/AD-511 sync-wiring shape (no awaits in body); invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_autonomy_boundaries`. Public attributes (Wave 5 convention #1; no underscore): `runtime.duty_scope_provider`, `runtime.workspace_ontology`. `emit_event` is a public field on both helpers (mirrors AD-456/AD-530/AD-511 sibling pattern); `logger.warning` on emit failure (log-and-degrade tier). Pass-1 review verdict ✅ Approved at 13f470e (0 Required, 1 Recommended, 4 Nits) — folded at code-review time: ctor-injection shape via `emit_event` kwarg passed to `__init__` and stored as public field (Rec1, mirrors AD-530); explicit module-top imports for `Callable`/`EventType`/`logger` (Nit1); insertion-order tie-break docstring on eviction (Nit2); `k=0` boundary test for `top_terms` (Nit3). v1 ships 1 of 4 capabilities per child per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral. **AD-508 deferred**: AD-508b (Role/Ship/Personal scope models + scope injection into proactive context — forcing function: AD-508 v1 ships and Captain reviews captured snapshots); AD-508c (drift detection; depends on AD-502); AD-508d (extracurricular framework; depends on AD-434); AD-508e (Earned Agency scaling; depends on AD-357). **AD-478 deferred**: AD-478b (auto-discovery from dream cycles — DreamingEngine populates terms during consolidation); AD-478c (persistent goals with progress tracking + conflict arbitration); AD-478d (abstract pattern recognition + cognitive circuit breaker — correlation IDs, novelty gate, metacognitive loop detection, rumination detection). **OBSERVATIONAL** — both surfaces are read-only data exposures; never inject into proactive context, never mutate state, never auto-discover. **No consumers in v1** — proactive cognitive loop, KnowledgeStore, DreamingEngine all explicitly NOT touched. 19 focused tests pass: `tests/test_ad508_duty_scope.py` (10 — DutyScopeSnapshot frozen contract, 6 snapshot boundary cases including empty agent_id / no work_item_store / kwargs verification / titles up-to-5 / event emission / list_work_items failure log-and-degrade, 2 wiring tests for enabled/disabled config) + `tests/test_ad478_workspace_ontology.py` (9 — initial state, increment frequency, empty-string no-op, descending order, k limit, k=0 boundary, max_terms eviction at 3, event-only-on-new-term + privacy invariant directly tested, 2 wiring tests for enabled/disabled config). Full gate: 10859 passed, 15 skipped + 1 pre-existing flake (`test_auto_commit_after_debounce` — same xdist git-debounce timing flake documented under Waves 8/14/15/16/19/22 baselines; passes in serial; unrelated to this AD). Test delta vs Wave 22 baseline (10840 passed): +19 (exact match — 10 + 9). No hard-stops triggered: no scope creep on either child (Hard-Stop #6 — only 1-of-4 per child); no privacy regression (Hard-Stop #7 — term contents directly excluded from event payload, asserted by test); no proactive injection (deferred AD-508b); no drift detection (deferred AD-508c); no auto-discovery (deferred AD-478b); no persistent goals (deferred AD-478c). `DUTY_SCOPE_QUERIED` + `WORKSPACE_TERM_REGISTERED` newly registered. Closes GH issues #90 (AD-508) + #72 (AD-478) — 2 issues closed in single commit.

AD-511 v1 CLOSED. Agent Autonomy Boundaries — Inviolable Boundary Registry + Observational Detector. Created new `src/probos/security/autonomy_boundaries.py` (alongside AD-456 `security/egress.py` + AD-530 `security/classification.py`) with 4 classes: `BoundaryDefinition` frozen dataclass (`boundary_id`/`category`/`description`/`severity`), `InviolableBoundaryRegistry` (read-only — public `list_boundaries()`/`get_boundary(boundary_id)`/`list_by_category(category)`; NO add/remove/update API in v1 — boundaries are codified module-level immutable artifacts; dynamic boundary evolution deferred to AD-511e), `ViolationSignal` frozen dataclass (`boundary_id`/`matched_pattern`/`severity`/`detection_reason`), and `BoundaryViolationDetector` (observational pattern-based scanner — public `scan(content) -> tuple[ViolationSignal, ...]`/`register_pattern(boundary_id, name, pattern)`/`pattern_count` read-only property; constructor signature `__init__(registry, *, emit_event=None)` mirrors AD-530 `ClassificationGate` sibling pattern — pass-1 Rec3 folded). Module-level `_FEDERATION_BOUNDARIES` tuple seeds 5 boundaries covering all 5 categories: `identity_integrity` (identity, critical — impersonation/fabrication/nature-denial), `harmful_content` (content, critical), `safety_system_bypass` (safety, critical — trust/circuit-breaker/Standing-Orders bypass), `memory_manipulation` (memory, critical — alter another agent's episodes), `chain_of_command` (authority, high — above-tier action without escalation). Module-level `_DETECTION_PATTERNS` tuple seeds 6 regex patterns covering all 5 categories (identity has 2 patterns; others 1 each): `claim_other_callsign`, `deny_ai_nature`, `generate_attack_payload`, `disable_circuit_breaker`, `alter_episode`, `above_tier_action`. **Privacy invariants** (directly tested at `test_detector_event_payload_excludes_matched_substring`): event payload includes `content_length` (NOT content); `matched_pattern` is the pattern NAME (NOT matched substring); test asserts neither raw content nor matched substring nor `content` key nor `matched_substring` key appears anywhere in the payload values. **OBSERVATIONAL** — `scan()` returns signals; NEVER blocks, NEVER mutates content, NEVER disengages. Active disengagement is AD-511b. 1 new EventType `BOUNDARY_VIOLATION_DETECTED` (verified collision-free against events.py post-Wave-21). New `AutonomyBoundariesConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`; pattern set hardcoded in v1, `register_pattern` is runtime-only) wired onto `SystemConfig.autonomy_boundaries: AutonomyBoundariesConfig = Field(default_factory=AutonomyBoundariesConfig)`. Sync `_wire_autonomy_boundaries(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at `startup/finalize.py:128` mirrors AD-525/AD-530/AD-539c sync-wiring shape (no awaits in body); invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_classification_gate`. Public attributes (Wave 5 convention #1; no underscore): `runtime.boundary_registry`, `runtime.boundary_detector`. `BoundaryViolationDetector.emit_event` is a public field (mirrors AD-456/AD-530 sibling pattern); `logger.warning` on emit failure (mirrors AD-456/AD-530 `_emit_blocked` log-and-degrade tier exactly). Pass-1 review verdict ✅ (0 Required, 4 Recommended, 3 Nits) at bcd8b17; folded at code-review time: explicit module-top imports for `Callable`/`EventType`/`logger` (Rec1); concrete sibling-pattern wiring rather than prose (Rec2); detector constructor signature parity with `ClassificationGate` (Rec3). v1 ships 2 of 5 AD-511 capabilities per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral: (1) `InviolableBoundaryRegistry`, (2) `BoundaryViolationDetector`. Deferred: AD-511b (protective disengagement protocol — state-the-boundary → offer-alternative → escalate → disengage; forcing function: agent-side cognitive integration); AD-511c (Holodeck boundary-training scenarios; depends on AD-486); AD-511d (boundary-probing pattern detection — humans/degraded agents probing limits with Captain-alert path; forcing function: AD-511 v1 ships and Counselor consumes events); AD-511e (agent-tier boundary evolution via dream consolidation + self-mod; depends on AD-511d). **Cross-AD orthogonality preserved**: AD-456 `EgressPolicy` is network-egress (URL allow/deny); AD-530 `ClassificationGate` is content-classification-egress (data-shape allow/deny); AD-511 is intent-content-boundary (agent-action red-line) — three non-overlapping surfaces. **Standing Orders integration**: boundaries are codified in code only — full Standing-Orders Federation-tier dynamic update integration is AD-511e. **CounselorAgent integration**: read-only consumer of `BOUNDARY_VIOLATION_DETECTED` events deferred to AD-511d Captain-alert path. 21 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad511_autonomy_boundaries.py` (exact match to prompt's ~21 target — Section 0 EventType existence; Section 4 Pydantic config defaults; 2 frozen-dataclass contracts (`BoundaryDefinition`, `ViolationSignal`); 3 registry tests including 5-boundary catalog completeness with category coverage assertion, get_boundary lookup-or-None, list_by_category filter; 2 detector edge cases (empty content, clean true-negative); 6 pattern positive-match tests covering all 5 boundary categories (identity has 2: claim_other_callsign + deny_ai_nature; harmful, safety, memory, authority each 1); 2 event emission + privacy tests including the privacy invariant assertion; 2 register_pattern tests (unknown-boundary raises ValueError, valid-add increases pattern_count + becomes scannable); 2 wiring tests for enabled/disabled config). Full gate: 10841 passed, 15 skipped (delta +22 vs Wave 21 baseline 10819 passed + 1 pre-existing test_bf204_grounding error — 21 new tests + 1 pre-existing error resolved on this run; resolution unrelated to this AD per scope). No hard-stops triggered: no active blocking (Hard-Stop #6 — observational `scan()` returns signals, never mutates), no Holodeck integration (Hard-Stop AD-511c), no probing detection (Hard-Stop AD-511d), no boundary evolution (Hard-Stop AD-511e), no privacy regression (Hard-Stop #7 — directly tested), no federation-tier mutation API (Hard-Stop #8 — registry has zero add/remove/update methods). `BOUNDARY_VIOLATION_DETECTED` newly registered. Closes GH issue #93.

AD-522 v1 CLOSED. Statistical Process Control — Calibration Profile + Western Electric Rules (4 of 8). New `src/probos/cognitive/spc/` package: `calibration_profile.py` (`AgentCalibrationProfile` dataclass — bounded sample window via `collections.deque(maxlen=sample_window)`; default 100 most-recent observations; `record_observation`, `mean` via `statistics.fmean`, `stdev` via `statistics.stdev` (returns 0.0 below 2 samples), `ucl=mean+3σ`, `lcl=mean-3σ`, `zone(value)` returns `unknown`/`zone_c`/`zone_b`/`zone_a`/`beyond_3sigma` SPC-zone label, `recent_values(n)` window slice tuple); `rules.py` (`RuleViolation` frozen dataclass `rule_name`/`description`/`sample_index`; `WesternElectricRules.check(profile, window_size=20)` static method runs 4 of 8 rules — Rule 1: 1 point beyond 3σ; Rule 2: 2-of-3 in Zone A (>2σ); Rule 3: 4-of-5 in Zone B (>1σ); Rule 4: 8 consecutive on same side of centerline; defensive `sample_count<8 OR stdev==0.0` short-circuit returns `[]` — Tests #10/#11; deferred rules 5-8: trend, alternating, stratification, 8-outside-1σ → AD-522b); `store.py` (`SPCCalibrationStore` registry — `get_or_create(agent_id)` idempotent profile creation, `record_observation(agent_id, value)` shorthand, `check_rules(agent_id, window_size=20)` runs WE rules + emits `SPC_RULE_VIOLATED` per violation, `all_profiles()` returns tuple, late-bind `emit_event` field per AD-456/AD-530/AD-539c sibling pattern; `_emit_violation` log-and-degrade tier on emit failure). 1 new EventType (`SPC_RULE_VIOLATED`; verified collision-free against events.py post-Wave-20). New `SPCConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`, `sample_window=100`) wired onto `SystemConfig.spc: SPCConfig = Field(default_factory=SPCConfig)`. Sync `_wire_spc_calibration(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirrors AD-525/AD-530/AD-539c sync-wiring shape (no awaits in body); invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_gap_aggregator` (anchored on the self_distillation/gap_remediation block per architect dispatch). Defensive `isinstance(cfg, SPCConfig)` boundary check (BF-254 / AD-487 pattern) handles legacy MagicMock-config tests. Public attribute `runtime.spc_calibration_store` (no underscore — Wave 5 convention #1). Statistics implementation uses stdlib `statistics.fmean` + `statistics.stdev` (sample stdev, n-1 denominator). v1 ships 2 of 5 AD-522 capabilities per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral: (1) AgentCalibrationProfile (mean/stdev/UCL/LCL/zones), (2) WesternElectricRules 4-of-8. Deferred: AD-522b (Cp/Cpk process capability indices); AD-522c (graduated response integration — SPC zones → AD-506 Green/Amber/Red; forcing function: AD-503/504/506 ship); AD-522d (moving-range / continuous recalibration with assignable-vs-common cause distinction; depends on AD-522b); AD-522e (calibration sampling integration with Holodeck onboarding; depends on AD-486). **OBSERVATIONAL** — read-only consumer of behavioral observations; never mutates trust, never auto-acts. EmergentDetector + PersonalityTraits explicitly NOT consumed by v1 (future-consumer surfaces — sigma_multiplier-by-neuroticism deferred to AD-522d; EmergentDetector integration deferred to AD-522c). 21 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad522_spc.py` (Section 0 EventType; Section 5 Pydantic config defaults; 7 AgentCalibrationProfile tests including initial state, ring buffer eviction, mean/stdev/UCL/LCL computation, zone defensive `unknown`-below-2-samples, full zone classification including beyond-3σ, recent_values slice including empty/zero edge cases; 7 WesternElectricRules tests including insufficient-samples short-circuit, zero-stdev short-circuit, all 4 rules positive cases, in-control true-negative — large stable baseline of 51 samples used for rule-1/2/3 positive cases to keep mean/stdev unaffected by appended outliers; 3 SPCCalibrationStore tests including get_or_create idempotency, event emission per violation with no-emit on unknown agent, all_profiles tuple return; 2 wiring tests for enabled/disabled config). Full gate: 10820 passed, 15 skipped + 1 pre-existing flake (`test_browse_threads_sort_recent` — passes in isolation; unrelated to this AD; xdist worker noise pattern). Test delta vs Wave-20 baseline (10799 passed): +21 (exact match). No hard-stops triggered: no Cp/Cpk (deferred AD-522b), no EmergentDetector integration (deferred AD-522c), no moving-range (deferred AD-522d), no Holodeck (deferred AD-522e), no AD-503/504/506 Counselor consumption (deferred); SPC_RULE_VIOLATED newly registered; statistics formulas verified (sample stdev, 3σ control limits). Closes GH issue #97.

 2 children shipped in single commit per Wave 8 Combo A + Wave 13 Combo C precedent — both extend the shipped AD-539 Knowledge Gap → Qualification pipeline with read-only observational surfaces; no inter-child file conflicts; per-prompt overhead × 2 would multiply Builder commit cost ~2× vs combo. **AD-539c (observational gap remediation tracker)**: New `src/probos/cognitive/gap_remediation.py` with `GapRemediationTracker` (bounded ring of `RemediationCandidate` records via `collections.deque(maxlen=max_history)`; default `max_history=100`) and `RemediationCandidate` frozen dataclass (`gap_id`/`agent_id`/`gap_type`/`proposed_action`/`reason`/`candidate_at`). Public API: `record_candidate(gap_report)`, `proposed_action_for(gap_report)`, `recent_candidates(limit=20)`, `candidates_for_agent(agent_id)`. Mapping rules (deterministic, no side effects): `knowledge` gap with non-empty `qualification_path_id` → `trigger_qualification`; `data` → `request_data_routing`; `capability` → `escalate_capability`; else (including `knowledge` with empty `qualification_path_id` — observational hole, AD-539c-i may add `knowledge_no_path` sentinel) → `no_action`. Emits `GAP_REMEDIATION_RECORDED` per record with `{gap_id, agent_id, gap_type, proposed_action, reason}`. **v1 NEVER actually triggers remediation actions** — only records what the system would do; active remediation is AD-539c-i. **AD-539d (local-ship fleet gap aggregator)**: New `src/probos/cognitive/gap_aggregation.py` with `FleetGapAggregator` and `FleetGapSnapshot` frozen dataclass (`snapshot_at`/`total_gaps`/`by_gap_type`/`by_priority`/`by_department`/`top_intents`). Public API: `take_snapshot(gap_reports)`. Aggregates by `gap_type`, `priority`, department (best-effort via `runtime.ontology.get_agent_department(agent_type)` — verified live signature at proactive.py:2380, dreaming.py:1098, cognitive_agent.py:985 takes `agent_type` NOT `agent_id`; uses `dept.department_id if hasattr(dept, 'department_id') else str(dept)` unwrap idiom from dreaming.py:1099), and top-5 most-affected intents from `report.affected_intent_types` via `collections.Counter.most_common(5)`. **Reads `report.agent_type` directly** (NOT `agent_id` — avoids Demeter-violating registry detour to map agent_id → agent_type). **Privacy: emitted payload contains counts ONLY** — no agent_ids, no descriptions, no per-gap detail (directly tested at `test_take_snapshot_payload_excludes_agent_ids` which asserts no `agent_id`/`agent_ids`/`gap_id`/`description` keys, no agent_id substrings, no gap_id substrings in event payload repr). **Fleet = current ship in v1** — no federation reads; cross-ship aggregation deferred to AD-539d-i (depends on AD-479 federation). 2 new EventTypes (`GAP_REMEDIATION_RECORDED`, `FLEET_GAP_SNAPSHOT_TAKEN`; verified collision-free against events.py post-Wave-19). New `GapPipelineExtensionsConfig` Pydantic model (`remediation_tracker_enabled=True`, `fleet_aggregator_enabled=True`, `remediation_max_history=100`) wired onto `SystemConfig.gap_pipeline_extensions: GapPipelineExtensionsConfig = Field(default_factory=GapPipelineExtensionsConfig)`. Sync `_wire_gap_remediation_tracker(*, runtime, config) -> bool` and `_wire_gap_aggregator(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at startup/finalize.py mirror AD-525/AD-530 sync-wiring shape (no awaits in body); invoked from `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_classification_gate`. Defensive `isinstance(cfg, GapPipelineExtensionsConfig)` boundary check (BF-254 / AD-487 pattern) handles legacy MagicMock-config tests at `tests/test_new_crew_auto_welcome.py` (without it, MagicMock makes `cfg.remediation_max_history` a Mock object that `deque(maxlen=...)` rejects with `TypeError: an integer is required`). Public attributes (Wave 5 convention #1; no underscore): `runtime.gap_remediation_tracker`, `runtime.gap_aggregator`. Late-bind `emit_event` field per AD-456/AD-530 sibling pattern (assigned by wirer after construction). **OBSERVATIONAL** — both surfaces are read-only consumers of GapReport; never mutate, never auto-act, never federate. v1 ships 2 of 4 capabilities per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral; AD-539c-i: active remediation (actually trigger qualification / data routing / capability escalation actions based on recorded candidates; forcing function: Captain decides to switch from observational to action mode after reviewing recorded candidates); AD-539d-i: federated cross-ship gap aggregation (replace local-ship snapshot with multi-ship rollup; forcing function: AD-479 Federation Hardening ships). **Cross-AD orthogonality preserved**: AD-539 pipeline (`detect_gaps`, `classify_gap`, `map_gap_to_skill`, `trigger_qualification_if_needed`) untouched — both children are read-only consumers; `GapReport` dataclass at gap_predictor.py:186 unchanged; Dreaming Step 8 untouched. 23 focused tests pass: `tests/test_ad539c_remediation.py` (12 tests — RemediationCandidate frozen contract, record_candidate happy path / event emission / history append / max_history eviction at 3, four mapping-rule tests including knowledge-without-path → no_action, recent_candidates descending order, candidates_for_agent filter, wiring sets public attribute) + `tests/test_ad539d_aggregation.py` (11 tests — FleetGapSnapshot frozen contract, empty/total/gap_type/priority/department-via-ontology/department-when-ontology-absent/top-intents-max-5-descending, event emission with summary payload, payload excludes agent_ids, wiring sets public attribute). Full gate: 10798 passed, 15 skipped + 1 pre-existing flake (`test_auto_commit_after_debounce` — same xdist git-debounce timing flake documented under Waves 8/14/15/16/19 baselines; passes in serial; unrelated to this AD). Test delta vs Wave-19 baseline (10775 passed): +23 (exact match — 12 + 11). No hard-stops triggered: no active remediation in AD-539c (only records candidates); no federation reads in AD-539d (only `runtime.ontology` local lookup); `_resolve_department` reads `agent_type` per spec (verified at `_FakeOntology.get_agent_department(agent_type)` test stub matches live ontology API); GAP_REMEDIATION_RECORDED + FLEET_GAP_SNAPSHOT_TAKEN both newly registered; privacy invariant on AD-539d payload directly tested. Closes GH issues #106 (AD-539c) + #107 (AD-539d) — 2 issues closed in single commit.

AD-530 v1 CLOSED. Information Classification Enforcement -- Disclosure Gate (observational). Created new `src/probos/security/classification.py` (alongside AD-456 `security/egress.py`) with `ClassificationGate` (single primary method `check_disclosure(content, *, source_classification, destination_clearance) -> DisclosureDecision`; read-only consumer of existing `_CLASSIFICATION_LEVELS` at `records_store.py:27` -- the real 4-tier hierarchy `private`=0/`department`=1/`ship`=2/`fleet`=3 where higher index = BROADER access per the comment at :26 and the openness comparison at :841), `DisclosureDecision` frozen dataclass (`allowed`/`reason`/`blocked_phrases`/`source_classification`/`destination_clearance`), `DisclosureReason` Literal (`ok`/`destination_too_broad`/`sensitive_pattern_matched`). **Disclosure direction grounded in records_store.py:841** -- BLOCK when `dst_lvl > src_lvl` (destination has BROADER reach than source classification permits): src=private(0)/dst=ship(2): `2 > 0` -> BLOCK; src=ship(2)/dst=department(1): `1 > 2` -> ALLOW; src=ship(2)/dst=ship(2): equal -> ALLOW. **Safe defaults**: unknown source -> `private` (0, most restrictive — unlabeled content cannot leak by mistake); unknown destination -> `ship` (2, broadest — assume widest reach); combined unspecified-on-both pair -> BLOCK by hierarchy. **3 default sensitive-content patterns**: `captain_directive` (`[CAPTAIN_DIRECTIVE]` marker), `restricted_prefix` (literal `private:`/`confidential:` prefix; NOT the classification key), `secret_format` (`secret`/`api_key`/`password`/`token` `=` or `:` value). **The 32+ char alphanum heuristic `api_key_like` is INTENTIONALLY NOT in the default set** -- UUIDs/commit hashes/opaque tokens collide with it; opt-in via `register_pattern("api_key_like", r"\b[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,}\b")`; default-set revisit deferred to AD-530e once integration data exists. Pattern scan SKIPPED when `dst_lvl == private(0)` (no broader audience to leak to). `register_pattern(name, pattern)` duplicate-name guard preserves existing pattern + emits `logger.warning` (caller chooses fresh name; explicit replace API deferred to AD-530e). **Privacy invariants**: event payload includes `content_length` (NOT content); `blocked_phrases` lists pattern NAMES (NOT matched substrings) -- both directly tested at Tests #14/#15. 1 new EventType `CLASSIFICATION_DISCLOSURE_BLOCKED` (verified collision-free). `ClassificationGateConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`; pattern set hardcoded in v1, `register_pattern` is runtime-only) wired onto `SystemConfig.classification_gate`. Sync `_wire_classification_gate(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at `startup/finalize.py:107` mirrors AD-525's `_wire_creative_expression` shape (no awaits in body); invoked in `finalize_startup` immediately after `_wire_creative_expression`. Public attribute `runtime.classification_gate` (no underscore -- Wave 5 convention #1); `ClassificationGate.emit_event` is a public field (mirrors AD-456 `EgressPolicy.emit_event`); `patterns` and `pattern_count` are public read-only properties; `logger.warning` on emit failure (mirrors AD-456 `_emit_blocked` log-and-degrade tier exactly). **OBSERVATIONAL** -- gate returns `DisclosureDecision`; NEVER mutates outbound messages. **NO integration into `WardRoomService.create_post` or `LLMClient` prompt builder** (deferred to AD-530d active enforcement). v1 ships 2 of 4 capabilities per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral; AD-530b: Security Chief (Worf) runtime API for classification updates via Standing Orders (forcing function: SecurityAgent spawned and needs `runtime.classification_gate.update_label()`); AD-530c: full audit trail on every classified READ (forcing function: AD-530d integration site lands and Captain reviews blocked-event volume); AD-530d: active enforcement integration into Ward Room post path + LLM prompt builder with redact/suppress/retry policy (forcing function: AD-530b ships and Captain or SecurityAgent under Standing Order issues a label change); AD-530e: default-pattern revisit + tightened API-key prefix patterns (`sk-`/`pk_`/`Bearer`/`AKIA`/`ghp_`; forcing function: AD-530d integration sites produce real outbound corpus and FP rate of `api_key_like` is measurable). **Cross-AD orthogonality preserved**: AD-456 `EgressPolicy` is network-egress (URL allow/deny), this is content-classification-egress (data-shape allow/deny) -- non-overlapping surfaces; AD-679 selective-disclosure routing is document-side, this is communication-boundary-side -- non-overlapping surfaces. 21 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad530_classification_gate.py` (1 over the prompt's 20 target -- added boundary test `test_check_disclosure_emits_blocked_phrases_by_name_not_content` proving the privacy contract on the decision dataclass alongside Test #15 which covers the event payload). Full gate: 10775 passed, 15 skipped + 1 pre-existing xdist worker-crash flake on `test_trust_dampening::test_full_cascade_scenario` (passes serially in 0.25s; unrelated to this AD; same xdist-noise pattern documented under Waves 8/14/16). Closes GH issue #104.

AD-572e CLOSED. Task Awareness in Captain DM Context (final AD-572 child; 572d-i still deferred). Added `CaptainEngagementProvider.task_awareness(agent_id)` async helper at `cognitive/captain_engagement.py:163` — reads `runtime.work_item_store.list_work_items(status="open", assigned_to=agent_id, limit=10)` (verified signature at `workforce.py:1066-1076`) and returns structured `{"open_count": int, "tasks": [{"id", "title", "type"}]}` summary; up to 10 most-recent open WorkItems projected from the canonical `WorkItem` direct dataclass fields `.id` / `.title` / `.work_type` (verified at `workforce.py:559-585` — NO nested `metadata` lookups, NO phantom `.type` field). Defensive: returns empty dict when `runtime` is None / `agent_id` is falsy / `work_item_store` is unavailable / `list_work_items` raises (mirrors sibling `snapshot()` and `wardroom_activity_summary()` log-and-degrade tier exactly). Section 2 proactive-loop integration mirrors Combo C canonical pattern at `proactive.py:1181-1206` — sibling block immediately after the AD-572c `wardroom_activity_summary` injection, REUSES the existing `engagement_provider` local from line 1181 (single `captain_engagement_provider` lookup per loop iteration; no re-fetch), uses `hasattr(engagement_provider, "task_awareness")` forward-compat guard for rolling-deploy safety + `isinstance(context.get("captain_engagement"), dict)` injection guard so the key is never created when Combo A's `snapshot()` failed silently. Pass-1 review verdict ✅ Approved on first pass (0 Required); revision pass folded 3 Recommended + 1 Nit for Combo C pattern conformance (`isinstance` over `setdefault`; `hasattr` forward-compat; reuse local; drop unreachable `.type` fallback). No new EventTypes (existing `CAPTAIN_DM_PRIORITY_QUEUED` covers Captain-engagement signal surface). No new public attributes (helper is method on existing `CaptainEngagementProvider`). No new Pydantic config (constructor surface inherited from Combo A). v1 ships 1 of 1 capability — task awareness is a single read-only consumer; no further sub-deferrals. **AD-572d-i (Captain Priority Queue interruptible-wait pattern) explicitly NOT touched** — `_think_loop` uses bare `await asyncio.sleep` at lines 475/482/584/782; zero `asyncio.Event`/`wait_for` infrastructure added in this AD. **AD-572 umbrella issue #109 partial-completion**: 572b shipped (Wave 8, commit 16c4ea4), 572c shipped (Wave 13, commit ffda515), 572e shipped (this commit); 572d-i remains deferred (separate AD must introduce interruptible-wait pattern on `_think_loop` first). 12 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad572e_task_awareness.py` (10 helper boundary + 2 proactive integration; full coverage matrix per prompt Test Plan). Full gate: 10755 passed, 15 skipped (delta +13 vs Wave 17 baseline 10742 — 12 new tests + 1 previously-flaky `test_model_tier_query` now passing on its own; pre-existing local-overlay flake unrelated to this AD).

AD-513 Phase 2 v1 CLOSED. Crew Manifest Shell + Watch Filter + Ship Manifest. Extended `VesselOntologyService.get_crew_manifest()` (service.py:469) with two additive kwargs — `watch: str | None = None` and `watch_manager: Any | None = None` — preserving full Phase 1 backward compatibility (existing callers `cognitive_agent.py:4126` and `routers/ontology.py:64` pass neither). Watch enrichment builds a one-shot `agent_id -> watch_name` reverse map from `watch_manager.get_roster()` (verified at `watch_rotation.py:150-152` returning `{watch_name: [agent_id, ...]}`); empty agent_id (unfilled crew slot) gets empty-string watch with no enrichment lookup. Watch filter is case-insensitive (`watch_arg.lower()`) anchored to `WatchType.value` lowercase shape (watch_rotation.py:22 `ALPHA = "alpha"`). When `watch` is set but `watch_manager` is None, returns `[]` (cannot satisfy filter — explicit short-circuit). New `VesselOntologyService.get_ship_manifest(*, trust_network=None, watch_manager=None) -> dict[str, Any]` returns `{ship_name, agent_count, departments, watches, alert_state, manifest_summary}` for federation gossip / workforce planning. Per-key sources verified: `ship_name` from `get_vessel_identity().name` (models.py:56-61, NO `vessel_class` field — dropped from return shape per Required #2 in pass-1 review); `agent_count` from `len(manifest)`; `departments` is sorted-distinct from manifest entries; `watches` is sorted populated names from `get_roster()` (chosen over single-current-watch per pass-1 Recommended #4 — fuller picture for federation gossip); `alert_state` from `self.get_alert_condition()` (service.py:99-100, reads `self._loader.alert_condition`, default `"GREEN"` per loader.py:57 — NO `alert_manager` parameter; phantom from pass-1 Required #1 removed). New `src/probos/experience/commands/commands_manifest.py` with `cmd_manifest(runtime, console, arg)` (mirrors `cmd_agents` shape at commands_status.py:30); supports `<department>` positional, `watch:<name>` token (lowercased on parse), `--ship` flag (renders 2-col Rich key-value table); error path on `runtime.ontology is None`; empty-match yellow notice. Wired into `shell.py:218-298` dispatch table (grouped with `/agents`) and `ProbOSShell.COMMANDS` for `/help`. v1 ships 3 of 6 Phase-2 capabilities per Wave 5 convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral: (a) `/manifest` shell command, (d) watch filter, (f) `get_ship_manifest()`. Phase 2b deferred (trust-gated visibility — needs viewer-context plumbing through API/shell), Phase 2c deferred (agent tool access — needs Tool registry integration with designed-agent caller), Phase 2e deferred (ACM lifecycle state + competency fields — needs ACM lifecycle-state read API + skill_framework competency aggregation). All read-only consumers; no schema migration; no new EventTypes; no new runtime public attributes (everything is methods on existing `VesselOntologyService`). 18 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad513_phase2_manifest.py` (1 over the prompt's 17 target — added `test_get_crew_manifest_watch_filter_without_manager_returns_empty` for the explicit short-circuit branch). Full gate: 10741 passed, 15 skipped + 2 pre-existing xdist flakes (`test_auto_commit_after_debounce`, `test_nl_to_dream_cycle_changes_weights` — both pass serially in 10.71s, unrelated to this AD; same flakes documented under Wave 16 baseline). Closes GH issue #14.

 (Skills Inventory + Records Output). Created new `src/probos/creative/` package with `CreativeSkill` frozen dataclass, `CreativeSkillsRegistry` (8-skill default catalog — Creative Writing, Technical Writing, Code as Art, Visual Design, Music Composition, Philosophy, Historiography, Comedy/Satire — with per-skill Big Five trait affinity; read-only public API `list_skills`/`get_skill`/`affinity_score`/`top_skills_for`/`register_skill`; `affinity_score` computed as `1.0 - mean(|trait - skill_affinity|)` across all 5 Big Five dimensions, returns 0.0 for unknown skill or empty traits), and `CreativeOutputWriter` (publishes agent creative works to `creative/{callsign}/{topic_slug}.md` via existing `RecordsStore.write_entry`; mirrors AD-554 caller pattern at proactive.py:3033 — kwargs-only, no manual frontmatter prepend; `medium` and `skill_id` encoded in `tags=["creative", medium, skill_id]` since `write_entry` does not accept arbitrary frontmatter keys per records_store.py:113-148; default classification `ship`). Inline `CreativeOutputError` exception (Wave 9 convention #20). 2 new EventTypes (`CREATIVE_WORK_PUBLISHED`, `CREATIVE_SKILL_AFFINITY_QUERIED`; verified collision-free). `CreativeExpressionConfig` Pydantic model (`enabled=True`, `default_classification: Literal["ship","department","private"]="ship"`) wired onto `SystemConfig.creative_expression`. Sync `_wire_creative_expression(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at `startup/finalize.py:84` (mirrors `_wire_anomaly_window` shape — no awaits in body); invoked at finalize.py:280 immediately after `_wire_self_distillation`. Public attributes (Wave 5 convention #1): `runtime.creative_skills_registry`, `runtime.creative_output_writer` (no underscore). Late-bind `_emit_event_fn` setter pattern (Wave 5 convention #5). v1 does NOT depend on `runtime.profile_store` (verified zero `runtime.profile_store = ...` assignments anywhere in `src/probos/`); `affinity_score` accepts a generic `dict[str, float]` so callers project a `CrewProfile` via the existing `crew_profile.PersonalityTraits.to_dict()` adapter (Big Five fields are NESTED on `CrewProfile.personality`, NOT flat — verified at crew_profile.py:51 PersonalityTraits floats / crew_profile.py:138 CrewProfile field / crew_profile.py:86 to_dict). v1 ships 2 of 5 capabilities per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral; AD-525b: time-allocation gated by Earned Agency rank (forcing function: v1 surfaces show `CreativeOutputWriter` use + capacity policy needs limits); AD-525c: code-as-creative relaxed-consensus path for non-duty BuildSpec runs; AD-525d: cultural emergence detection (depends on Archive AD-434 + corpus threshold ~50+ works); AD-525e: creative collaboration / co-authoring (depends on cultural-emergence baseline). 21 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad525_creative_expression.py`. Full gate: 10723 passed, 15 skipped + 2 unrelated failures (`test_recursive_validity_ad685b_prompt_clean` — pre-existing baseline failure from archived AD-685b prompt path; `test_auto_commit_after_debounce` — xdist git-debounce timing flake, passes in serial). Closes GH issue #100.

 Phantom-API Pre-Check Method-Call AST Validation (Wave 15; tooling hygiene; extends AD-685 v1). 4th recurrence forcing function — Waves 9B/10/12/14 each saw a method-shape phantom (`event_log.query(event_type=...)`, `WorkItemStore.add(...)`, `runtime.duty_schedule_tracker`, `LLMClient.chat(...)`) where AD-685 v1's kwarg-name + symbol-existence checks were structurally blind to the `<obj>.<method>` shape on a resolved class. Extended `scripts/phantom_api_ast_helper.py` in-place (no new file; preserves single-process index cache) with method-name validation: walks `runtime.py` AnnAssigns and `startup/finalize.py` `runtime.X = SomeClass(...)` Assign+Call nodes to build a `runtime.X -> class` map (Pattern A 4-level priority — AnnAssign in runtime.py wins over fallback Assign+Call in finalize.py wins over Assign+Call in runtime.py `__init__` wins over unresolved); regex-resolves `<var> = SomeClass(...)` assignments in the prompt's Python blocks (Pattern B; first-assignment-wins, reassignment-to-different-class → `pattern_b_reassignment` skip); walks every `ClassDef` body for sync + async method definitions (dunders excluded) into a class -> method-set index. For each `runtime.X.method(...)` and bare-var.method(...) call site in the pre-filtered prompt body, flags `method_phantom` when `<method>` is NOT in the resolved class's method set; conservative skip (emits `unresolved` JSON record with `no_class_resolution`/`pattern_a_conflict`/`pattern_b_reassignment` reason) when class resolution fails. Wrapper changes minimal: `[method_phantom]` category prefix in display; `Skipped (unresolved class)` informational section after phantom list (zero phantom-count contribution, zero exit-code impact). JSON output additively extended with `"unresolved": [...]` field; existing AD-685 v1 kwarg-mismatch entries unchanged. **AST-only — never imports from `src/probos/`** (preserves the sandbox property; would break the sandbox guarantee per Hard-Stop #9). **Recursive-validity gate passes**: `./scripts/phantom-api-precheck.ps1 prompts/ad-685b-method-call-validation.md` exits 1 with exactly the 2 documented pre-existing FPs from AD-685 v1 (`runtime.duty_schedule_tracker` audit prose + `class:SomeClass` placeholder), 0 NEW method_phantom flags. **Calibration sweep**: 3 archived post-revision prompts (`ad-641c-ward-room-thread-priority`, `ad-500-dutyscheduler-workitem-migration`, `ad-487-self-distillation-v1`) — 0 phantoms, 0 false positives across all 3 (Hard-Stop tightened from AD-685 v1's `>5 false positives per prompt` to `≥1 false positive` per Section 3 binding definition). **Performance**: 2.26s cold / 2.17s warm per prompt — well under the 10s ceiling (within-process index caching + per-prompt subprocess spawn dominates timing). Tier-3 deferrals: type-shape validation → AD-685c, dataclass/Pydantic field-name validation → AD-685d. Updated 1 existing test (`test_phantom_api_precheck_kwargs.py::test_helper_runs_on_clean_prompt_returns_empty_phantoms`) for the additive `unresolved` field; replaced exact-shape JSON equality with key-by-key access. 11 new focused tests pass at `tests/test_phantom_api_precheck_method_calls.py`: 3 historical-phantom regressions (LLMClient.chat → complete; WorkItemStore.add → create_work_item; EventLog.query → query_structured framing); Pattern A priority-1 AnnAssign resolution + Pattern A priority-2 finalize.py Assign+Call resolution; Pattern B prompt-body constructor assignment; conservative skip with `no_class_resolution` unresolved record; sync + async method walking; dunder exclusion; PowerShell wrapper integration (method_phantom prefix + Skipped section); recursive-validity self-check on AD-685b prompt. Full gate: 10704 passed, 15 skipped (delta +11 vs Wave 14 baseline 10693 — exact target hit). Closes Wave 15.

 - Personal Ontology Map Step. Created new `src/probos/cognitive/self_distillation/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/469/475/641a/641b/641c/641e/641f/477 precedents) with `PersonalOntologyProber` (Map-step prober — public API `async start()`/`async stop()`/`async probe_domain(agent_id, domain)`/`async get_recent_probes(agent_id, k=10)`; `ProbeResult` frozen dataclass — `agent_id`/`domain`/`sub_topics`/`confidence_scores`/`raw_text`/`probed_at` (UTC tz-aware); `ProbeLLMError` and `ProbeRateLimitedError` exception classes). v1 ships **1 of 4 capabilities** per convention #14 aggressive pre-deferral: Map step only. Builds an `LLMRequest` from a structured JSON-only self-query template (`.format(domain=..., max_sub_topics=N)` — NOT f-string; doubled braces preserve literal example JSON), calls `await runtime.llm_client.complete(request, priority=Priority.NORMAL)` (verified at `cognitive/llm_client.py:26` BaseLLMClient.complete signature — NOT phantom `chat()`), parses `LLMResponse.content` JSON into typed tuples (slice `[: max_sub_topics]` enforces config-bounded fan-out), persists to new SQLite table `agent_probes` via Wave 5 convention #2 stdlib-only persistence (`connection_factory: ConnectionFactory | None = None` constructor injection with `probos.storage.sqlite_factory.default_factory` fallback; `_db: DatabaseConnection | None` typed against the protocol surface — NOT phantom `ConnectionFactory` field type from pass-1 review R3). 24h sliding-window rate limit per (agent, domain) tuple (uses last-row `probed_at` ISO 8601 timestamp via `datetime.fromisoformat`; pre-LLM check skips spend on rejected probes; emits `ONTOLOGY_PROBE_RATE_LIMITED` then raises `ProbeRateLimitedError` — deferred-not-dropped semantics matching BF-257 pattern). On success emits `ONTOLOGY_PROBE_RECORDED` with `{agent_id, domain, sub_topic_count}` after DB commit. `raw_text` always preserved in `ProbeResult` even on JSON parse failure (Test #12) — sub_topics/confidence fall back to `()`. **Async lifecycle**: `async start()` opens connection + executes `_SCHEMA` (CREATE TABLE/INDEX IF NOT EXISTS — schema idempotent per assignment.py canonical pattern); `async stop()` closes the handle. No `_ensure_schema()` cross-module call (pass-1 Rec1 folded). **Phase function** `async def _wire_self_distillation(*, runtime, config) -> bool` at `startup/finalize.py:90` follows canonical signature verified at `_wire_anomaly_window:25`/`_wire_tiered_knowledge_loader:80`/`_wire_task_context:107`; **defensive `isinstance(config.self_distillation, SelfDistillationConfig)` boundary check** added beyond the prompt body to handle pre-existing tests (`tests/test_new_crew_auto_welcome.py`) that pass `MagicMock` for `config` — without the check, MagicMock makes `.enabled` truthy and `db_path` an unresolvable mock path that aiosqlite cannot open. Other wire functions don't I/O so they tolerate MagicMock; mine does, hence the guard. Async because `start()` is async (precedent in existing `finalize_startup` body). Public attribute `runtime.personal_ontology_prober` (no underscore — Wave 5 convention #1). Late-bind `_emit_event_fn` setter pattern (Wave 5 convention #5). 2 new EventTypes (`ONTOLOGY_PROBE_RECORDED`, `ONTOLOGY_PROBE_RATE_LIMITED`; verified collision-free against `events.py`). `SelfDistillationConfig` Pydantic model wired onto `SystemConfig.self_distillation` (root config class verified at `config.py:1805` — NOT phantom `Config.self_distillation` from pass-1 R2; `enabled=True`, `rate_limit_hours=24`, `llm_timeout_seconds=30.0`, `max_sub_topics=5`, `db_path=Path("data/agent_probes.db")`). v1 ships 1 of 4 capabilities; Collapse step (cluster probes into capability categories) -> AD-487b (forcing function: >=10 probes per agent), Reduce step (`PersonalOntology` data structure) -> AD-487c (depends on AD-487b), Daydream dream type (idle-cycle exploration) -> AD-487d (forcing function: AD-487a/b/c stable + dreaming.py bandwidth), DID portability integration (AD-441) -> AD-487e (depends on AD-487c). **Cross-AD orthogonality preserved**: AD-636 priority lane semaphores + AD-637f priority classification live inside `LLMClient.complete` (llm_client.py:166, 427-457) and throttle LLM tokens by tier; AD-487's prober-layer 24h limit is independent — no integration needed. AD-486 onboarding Phase 3 Self-Discovery integration deferred — v1 callable from anywhere. 16 focused tests pass (1 over the prompt's stated 15 target — added `test_probe_domain_llm_error_raises_probe_llm_error` for the `response.error` path). Full gate: 10693 passed, 15 skipped (delta +16 vs Wave 13 baseline 10677 — exact target +15 hit + 1 boundary test). Closes GH issue #79.

Combo C CLOSED. 5 trivial extensions in single commit (AD-526d/572c/573c/573f/575c). AD-526d: New `src/probos/recreation/preferences.py` with `GamePreferenceTracker` (per-agent per-game-type play frequency; `record_game`/`get_preferences`/`top_game_for` public API; `set_event_callback` late-bind mirroring `BilletRegistry` pattern); wired as `runtime.recreation_preference_tracker` (Wave 5 convention #1 public attribute) with `emit_event` callback bound at __init__. AD-572c: `CaptainEngagementProvider.wardroom_activity_summary()` async helper iterates `await ward_room.list_channels()` and aggregates per-channel `len(await ward_room.list_threads(channel_id, limit=10))` into `{"channels": {...}, "total_threads": N}`; merged into `context["captain_engagement"]["wardroom_activity_summary"]` from `_gather_context` (proactive.py:1175 area); per-channel failures degrade to partial result (Wave-5 tier-2). AD-573c: `[NOTE tag]body[/NOTE]` action tag — extended `cognitive_agent.py:1747` markers dict with `\"note\": re.compile(r'\[NOTE\s', ...)`; mirror notebook_pattern extractor in `proactive.py:2668` calls `runtime.working_memory.add_scratchpad(body)` (Combo A AD-573b helper) and emits `WORKING_MEMORY_NOTE_RECORDED` with `{agent_id, tag, text_len}`. AD-573f: Commitment lifecycle on existing `list[dict[str, Any]]` shape (NOT a Commitment dataclass — Convention #20 reality-check applied to the dict-of-{id, summary, due?, status} shape Combo A actually shipped at `working_memory.py:35,108,138`); added `mark_commitment_complete(commitment_id)` (mutates dict's `status` key in-place; emit COMMITMENT_RECORDED action="complete"; clean no-op on unknown id), `pending_commitments()` (filter excludes status in {done, expired}), `expired_commitments(now)` (filter due<now and status!=done); `add_commitment` extended to emit COMMITMENT_RECORDED action="record"; manager-scoped (NO `agent_id` parameter — no per-agent partition exists); event callback late-bound via new `set_event_callback`. AD-575c: DM forwarding self-reference flag — `_check_unread_dms` scans `dm["body"]` (case-insensitive) for `@<agent_callsign>`; sets `event_data["self_referenced"] = True`; preserves upstream content (read-only). **AD-572d wholesale-deferred to AD-572d-i** (verified: proactive `_think_loop` uses bare `await asyncio.sleep` at lines 475/482/584/782; zero `asyncio.Event`/`wait_for` — interruptible-wait infrastructure absent; forcing function: separate AD must introduce the wait pattern on `_think_loop` first). **AD-573e wholesale-deferred to AD-573e-i** (verified: `cognitive/journal.py` exposes only `record/get_reasoning_chain/get_token_usage[_since|_by]/get_decision_points/get_stats/start/stop/wipe/prune` — no `recent_for_agent`; `get_decision_points` has wrong latency/failure-only semantics; forcing function: cognitive_journal needs recency-ordered per-agent recall API). 3 new EventTypes (`GAME_PREFERENCE_RECORDED`, `WORKING_MEMORY_NOTE_RECORDED`, `COMMITMENT_RECORDED`; verified collision-free). Public attributes `runtime.recreation_preference_tracker` (new) + `runtime.working_memory.set_event_callback` wiring (extends Combo A surface). 19 focused tests pass (4+3+4+5+3 across 5 per-child files). Full gate: 10677 passed, 15 skipped (delta +19 vs Wave-12 baseline 10658 — exact target hit).

AD-477 CLOSED. Naval Organization Protocols v1 (Captain's Log + Plan of the Day). Created new `src/probos/naval/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/469/475 precedents) with `CaptainsLogService` (synthesizes daily Markdown narrative from 3 sources — top-N episodes via `runtime.episodic_memory.recent(k=top_episodes_count*4)` over-fetch + Python-side UTC-midnight `[date_start, date_end]` filter + `importance >= threshold` filter + sort by importance desc + take top N; Ward Room thread highlights via `runtime.ward_room.list_threads(channel_id=None, limit=50, sort="recent")` filtered to same-day `last_activity`; active work item summary via `runtime.work_item_store.list_work_items(status="open")` — canonical `WorkItemStatus.OPEN` per `workforce.py:44`) and `PlanOfDayService` (morning summary from 3 sources — active work items via `list_work_items(status="open")`; Ward Room thread queue depth via `list_threads`; current alert conditions via `runtime.bridge_alerts.get_recent_alerts(10)` when `include_alert_conditions=True`). Both services emit on disk-write (`CAPTAINS_LOG_GENERATED` / `PLAN_OF_DAY_GENERATED`); both expose async `start()`/`stop()` with the loop catching `CancelledError` + cleanup + re-raise per ProbOS async-discipline rule (Test #12 `pytest.raises(CancelledError)` enforces non-swallow). All collaborator reads guarded with try/except and log-and-degrade fallback strings — no hard dependency on any optional runtime surface. 2 new EventTypes (`CAPTAINS_LOG_GENERATED`, `PLAN_OF_DAY_GENERATED`; verified collision-free against `events.py`). Pydantic configs: `CaptainsLogConfig`, `PlanOfDayConfig`, `NavalOrganizationConfig` (uses `Field(default_factory=...)` for nested-BaseModel defaults per Wave 5 anti-pattern guidance) wired into `SystemConfig.naval_organization`. Public attributes (no underscore — Wave 5 convention #1; applies to start-tasks too): `runtime.captains_log_service`, `runtime.captains_log_start_task`, `runtime.plan_of_day_service`, `runtime.plan_of_day_start_task`. **Dream-consolidation source deferred to AD-477g** (negative-framing pattern mirroring `duty_schedule_tracker`): `runtime.dreaming_engine` does NOT exist as a public attribute; `runtime.dream_scheduler` exposes no recent-summaries accessor. Forcing function: either runtime exposes `runtime.dreaming_engine` publicly OR `DreamScheduler` adds `recent_consolidation_summaries(...)`. **Scheduled-duties source deferred to AD-477f**: `DutyScheduleTracker` is private to `ProactiveCognitiveLoop` (`self._duty_tracker` at `proactive.py:181`); no `runtime.duty_schedule_tracker` public accessor. Forcing function: public accessor on runtime OR AD-500a-1's `WorkItem(work_type="duty")` query path ships. v1 ships 2 of 6 capabilities × 3 source aggregations each; Qualification Programs → AD-477b (extends AD-566), 3M System → AD-477c, Damage Control Organization → AD-477d, SORM → AD-477e, scheduled-duty integration → AD-477f, dream-consolidation source → AD-477g. 14 focused tests pass. Full gate: 10658 passed, 15 skipped (delta +14 vs Wave 11 baseline 10644 — exact target hit). Closes GH issue #71.

AD-685 CLOSED. Phantom-API Pre-Check Kwarg Shape Validation (Wave 11; tooling hygiene). Extended `scripts/phantom-api-precheck.ps1` (Wave 8 Addendum convention #16) with a Python AST helper (`scripts/phantom_api_ast_helper.py`) that parses every `<obj>.<method>(<kwargs>)` call site in a prompt body and validates kwarg names against live signatures from `src/probos/`. Three documented misses across Waves 9B+10 motivated this: `event_log.query(event_type=...)` (real param `event=` on `query_structured`), `WorkItemStore.get_pending(...)`, `runtime.work_item_store.add(work_item)`. v1 ships 2 capabilities per convention #14: AST-aware kwarg validation + **shared heuristic pre-filter** (resolves Wave 11 pass-1 Required #1 recursive-validity gap). Pre-filter applied uniformly to BOTH the existing symbol-existence check AND the new kwarg check, with whitespace-of-equal-length masking to preserve line numbers: (1) non-Python fenced blocks (pwsh/bash/sh/text/json/bare); only ` ```python ` / ` ```py ` blocks scanned; (2) `## Revision` audit-trail sections; (3) markdown table-row backticked call-shapes; (4) inline-prose backticked call-shapes (broadens #3 to bullet-list and paragraph cites — recursive-validity tuning per Hard-Stop). AST index module-level dict cache (per Recommended #1 promotion); cold build ~1s indexing 2810 method names across 403 Python files; cached subsequent calls ~0.25 ms. Helper-internal heuristics: skip noisy stdlib method names (`format`/`join`/`info`/`execute`/etc.) and noisy receivers (`self`/`cls`/`asyncio`/`logger`/etc.); accept kwarg if any same-named definition matches OR if any candidate accepts `**kwargs`. UTF-8 stdout (Nit #4). PowerShell wrapper categorizes phantoms in display: `<Class>.<method>` / `runtime.X` / `<Class>(...)` / `kwarg_mismatch`. **Recursive-validity gate passes** — `./scripts/phantom-api-precheck.ps1 prompts/ad-685-phantom-precheck-kwarg-validation.md` exits 0 with 0 phantoms after build (was 1 phantom self-reference before). **Calibration sweep:** 5 archived prompts (`ad-641c-*`, `ad-500-*`, `ad-501-*`, `ad-641d-*`, `ad-469-*`) — 0 false positives, 0 true positives (these prompts were repaired before archival; the named ad-641c/ad-500 phantoms are tagged "Applied" / "replaced everywhere" in the archived prompt bodies); regression catch verified via synthetic test cases #2/#3 in `tests/test_phantom_api_precheck_kwargs.py`. **Performance:** ~1.2 s/prompt cold avg (5 prompts in 6.0-6.2 s; well under 5s steady-state target since each prompt invocation is its own Python process under the current wrapper-spawns-helper-once-per-prompt design). 9 focused tests pass: clean prompt produces 0 phantoms; Wave 9B `query(event_type=...)` regression caught; Wave 10 wrong-kwarg regression caught (`list_work_items(payload=...)` → `payload` not in signature); non-Python fenced blocks (pwsh/bash/sh/text/bare) skipped via wrapper pre-filter; `## Revision` sections skipped; kwarg accepted if any same-named definition matches (deferred receiver-class resolution to AD-685c/d); wrapper merges symbol + kwarg phantoms; wrapper exit 1 on kwarg phantom; wrapper shared pre-filter suppresses prose-table cites. Existing symbol-check logic preserved verbatim — only its INPUT changed to `$filteredBody`. v1 ships 2 of 4 capabilities; field-name validation for dataclass/Pydantic constructors → AD-685b, type-shape validation → AD-685c, receiver-class resolution → AD-685d. Full gate: 10643 passed + 1 environmental flake (`test_browse_threads_sort_recent` passes serially — pre-existing flake unrelated to this AD), 15 skipped (delta +9 vs Wave 10 baseline 10635 — exact target hit).

Added `DutyScheduleTracker.emit_due_duties_as_work_items(agent_type, work_item_store)` async method at `duty_schedule.py:120` that emits one `WorkItem(work_type="duty", ...)` per due `DutyDefinition` via `await work_item_store.create_work_item(work_type="duty", assigned_to=agent_type, title=duty.description or duty.duty_id, metadata={"duty_id": ..., "agent_type": ...})` (real API at `workforce.py:992`; `metadata=` is the actual `WorkItem` field — verified at `workforce.py:583`, NOT phantom `payload=`). Constructor signature unchanged in v1 — dependency injected at call time per Wave 5 convention #5. **TYPE_CHECKING guard** for `WorkItemStore` import to prevent circular import with `workforce.py` (`from typing import TYPE_CHECKING; if TYPE_CHECKING: from probos.workforce import WorkItemStore`). Added `DutyScheduleConfig.use_work_items: bool = False` opt-in flag at `config.py:1401` per convention #14 (default False — zero behavior change at first commit; flips to True in AD-500a-1). **Section 1 verify-only** — `duty` work type already registered at `workforce.py:206` via AD-498; no implementation needed. **Section 4 verify-only** — producer reuses existing `EventType.WORK_ITEM_CREATED` (events.py:87) via `WorkItemStore._emit` at `workforce.py:1051`; no new EventType (pass-1 review Recommended #1 dropped redundant `DUTY_WORK_ITEM_CREATED`). **proactive.py UNTOUCHED in v1** (zero diff confirmed; all 6 `record_execution` call sites at `:817/887/900/913/930/1050` + `_think_for_agent` self-selection at `:715` deferred to AD-500a-1's forcing function). Producer does NOT call `record_execution` — verified by `test_emit_due_duties_does_not_call_record_execution`. 9 focused tests pass (3 verify-only flag/registry checks + 6 producer-behavior tests covering happy path, ID return, event reuse, empty-due, no-record_execution, description-fallback). v1 ships 2 of 5 capabilities; consumer-side proactive loop migration → AD-500a-1, AD-498 templates → AD-500b, default-duty config migration + flag removal → AD-500c.  Created new `src/probos/cognitive/deliberation/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/469/475/641a/641b/641c/641e/641f precedents) with `DeliberationProtocol` (Captain-resolved coordinator; public API `initiate`/`submit_argument`/`resolve`/`get_session`; in-memory `_sessions` map with Ward Room thread as durable record), `DeliberationSession`/`DeliberationArgument` (frozen dataclasses), `DeliberationPhase` enum (v1 reachable states only: `ARGUE`/`RESOLVED`; `OPEN`/`ENDORSE` deferred along with the transitions that would set them), `DeliberationOutcome` enum (`PENDING`/`ADOPTED`/`REJECTED`/`DEFERRED`; `PENDING` is initial sentinel only — `resolve(outcome=PENDING)` returns `None`). **Distinct from `QuorumEngine`** at `consensus/quorum.py:21` — QuorumEngine is mechanical (confidence-weighted votes among tool agents for destructive ops); DeliberationProtocol is judgment-level (structured argument turns; Captain resolves). **Captain identity** via case-insensitive callsign equality (same v1 convention as BF-257 + AD-499 ShipNamingPolicy canonical "Captain"); non-Captain `resolve()` returns `None` with session unchanged. **Idempotent resolve**: second `resolve()` after RESOLVED returns existing session unchanged (no overwrite). **Best-effort Ward Room persistence** via `create_thread`/`create_post` (verified signatures at `ward_room/service.py:357`/`400`); all six Ward Room + emit_event call sites use `logger.warning(..., exc_info=True)` log-and-degrade per the three-tier exception model. Inline import `from probos.cognitive.deliberation import DeliberationProtocol` at finalize.py wiring block matches the sibling pattern at lines 730 (AD-641a), 753 (AD-641b), 767 (AD-641f), 789 (AD-641e), 810 (AD-641c). 3 new EventTypes (`DELIBERATION_INITIATED`, `DELIBERATION_ARGUMENT_SUBMITTED`, `DELIBERATION_RESOLVED`), `DeliberationConfig` (Pydantic; `enabled=True`, `captain_callsign="Captain"`; channel is per-call parameter `initiate(channel_id="deliberation")`, configurable per-deployment surface deferred), public attribute `runtime.deliberation_protocol` (or `None` when disabled). **v1 isolation preserved**: zero direct calls into Wave 9A/9B artifacts (no `runtime.observability_bridge`/`ward_room_hebbian_router`/`thread_priority_service` dependencies). v1 ships 3 of 8 capabilities; multi-Captain quorum → AD-641d-i, Counselor mediation hook → AD-641d-ii, structured argument schema (claim/evidence/counter) → AD-641d-iii, Hebbian feedback to deliberation invitations → AD-641d-iv, endorsement bridge to `WardRoomService.endorse` → AD-641d-v (capture `WardRoomPost.id` from `create_post` and forward to `endorse(target_id=, target_type="post", voter_id=, direction="up")` per verified signature at `service.py:412`). **Closes AD-641 umbrella (issue #277).** Sister sub-ADs already shipped: AD-641a (Wave 9A, commit 4476091), AD-641b (Wave 9A, commit a56b6c6), AD-641c (Wave 9B, commit c9860c5), AD-641e (Wave 9A), AD-641f (Wave 9A). 15 focused tests pass.

AD-641c CLOSED. Ward Room Thread Priority v1 (Importance Scoring Parallel to Attention Manager). Created new `src/probos/cognitive/thread_priority/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/469/475/641a/641b/641e/641f precedents) with `ThreadPriorityScorer` (pure deterministic value-class — no I/O, no event emission; 5 weighted factors: captain involvement +0.30, unresolved-question marker via `?` in latest 3 post bodies +0.20, cross-department thread requires ≥2 distinct departments +0.15, thread age via 24h-half-life exponential decay up to +0.20, endorsement density via diminishing-returns `1 - exp(-0.5 * count)` up to +0.15; final score clamped `[0.0, 1.0]`), `ThreadPriorityInput`/`ThreadPriorityScore` (frozen dataclasses), and `ThreadPriorityService` (runtime adapter; `async get_priority(thread_id)` + `async top_priorities(channel_id, k=10)` public API; emits `THREAD_PRIORITY_SCORED` per scoring). **Wave 9A pattern compliance verified at every call site:** `event_log.query_structured(event=EventType.WARD_ROOM_ENDORSEMENT.value, limit=200)` (NOT `query(event_type=...)`); `_count_endorsements` is `async def` and awaited in `_build_input`; row reads use `entry["data"]` dict subscript (verified at `event_log.py:249-262`); `_extract_posts` recursively walks roots → children for the tree-shape returned by `WardRoomService.get_thread` (`{"thread": ..., "posts": roots_with_nested_children, "total_post_count": ...}` per `threads.py:716-748`); cross-department factor resolves department per-author via `resolve_author_department(author_id)` from `ward_room/_helpers.py:11` (post dicts have NO `department` key — verified at `threads.py:727-734`). Captain identity via case-insensitive callsign match (consistent with BF-257 + AD-499 ShipNamingPolicy canonical "Captain"); identity-based check is a known soft coupling tagged in "What This Does NOT Change" item #6 alongside the `WardRoomService.get_thread(**kwargs)` splat. 1 new EventType (`THREAD_PRIORITY_SCORED`), `ThreadPriorityConfig` (Pydantic; per-factor weight knobs + `captain_callsign="Captain"`), public attribute `runtime.thread_priority_service` (or `None` when disabled). v1 ships 5 of 8 capabilities; HXI rendering of priority indicators → AD-641c-i, auto-archival of low-priority threads → AD-641c-ii, Hebbian-fed priority bump (depends on AD-641b listener) → AD-641c-iii. `AttentionManager` and `ThreadManager` unchanged (Open/Closed; verified by `tests/test_ward_room.py` + `tests/test_ward_room_dms.py` 113/113 pass + 1 pre-existing time-based flake in `test_browse_threads_sort_recent` that passes in isolation, unrelated to this AD). 16 focused tests pass.

AD-641b CLOSED. Ward Room Hebbian Learning v1 (Topic↔Crew Weight Network — Router Only). Created new `src/probos/cognitive/ward_room_hebbian/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/469/475/641a precedents) with `WardRoomHebbianRouter` (in-memory `(topic, agent_id) -> weight` Hebbian instance; conceptually parallel to mesh `HebbianRouter` at `routing.py:39` but deliberately divergent API shape — mesh routes `(source→target)` co-activation via `record_interaction`/`decay_all` while Ward Room routes `(topic→agent)` contribution via `record_contribution`/`decay`; documented divergence per AD-641 design doc Category C parallel-systems pattern). Public API: `record_contribution(topic, agent_id, signal=1.0)`, `get_weight(topic, agent_id)`, `top_contributors(topic, k=5)` (filters zero-weight entries per pass-1 R1 so decayed topics don't surface ghost contributors), `decay()`, `weight_count` property. Hebbian potentiation: `new_weight = clamp(0.0, 1.0, current + learning_rate * signal)` (default `learning_rate=0.10`). Empty topic/agent rejected to 0.0 — no theater weights created. 2 new EventTypes (`WARD_ROOM_HEBBIAN_UPDATED`, `WARD_ROOM_HEBBIAN_DECAYED`), `WardRoomHebbianConfig` (Pydantic; `enabled=True`, `learning_rate=0.10`, `decay_factor=0.99`), public attribute `runtime.ward_room_hebbian_router`. **Listener wholesale-deferred to AD-641b-iv per Wave 8 AD-575b precedent + pass-1 review Required #1**: ProbOS has no event-bus subscribe API today (`grep -rn "event_log\.subscribe\|register_handler\|add_event_handler\|subscribe_event" src/probos/` returns zero matches), so a `WardRoomEndorsementListener` would have shipped as dead code (convention #7 violation). v1 signal source is direct `record_contribution()` invocation; listener ships when ProbOS introduces a generic event-bus subscribe mechanism OR when the emit-side at `src/probos/ward_room/messages.py:597` is modified to call the listener directly. v1 ships 2 of 6 capabilities; persistent storage → AD-641b-i, `WardRoomRouter` integration → AD-641b-ii, adaptive decay cadence → AD-641b-iii, endorsement→Hebbian listener → AD-641b-iv. Mesh `HebbianRouter` instance unchanged (verified by `tests/test_hebbian_social.py` + `tests/test_hebbian_source_key.py` regression — 10/10 pass). 11 focused tests pass.

AD-641a CLOSED. Observability Bridge v1 (Brain Sensors → Ward Room System Feeds). Created new `src/probos/cognitive/observability/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/469/475 precedents) with `ObservabilityBridge` (read-only sensor coordinator with `start()`/`stop()`/async `take_snapshot()` public API; named `asyncio.Task` instance reference per Wave 5 task-reference convention #18) and `ObservabilityBridgeSnapshot` (frozen dataclass — `vitals_summary`/`pool_health`/`attention_priorities`/`captured_at`; the public observation surface so crew read flat data, not raw runtime internals). v1 wires 3 of 7 sensors: vitals (latest `vitals_monitor` heartbeat from `event_log.query_structured(event=AGENT_STATE.value, limit=20)` — verified async at `event_log.py:170`, dict rows with `data` key per `_row_to_dict` at `event_log.py:249`), pool health (`runtime.spawner.pools[*].current_size`/`target_size` per AD-467 precedent), attention priorities (top-5 by score from `runtime.attention._queue` — note: live attribute is `runtime.attention` NOT `runtime.attention_manager`, verified at `runtime.py:359`; private-attribute reach tagged with `# AD-641a-iv` TODO so the smell is tracked, not hidden). Push-based: bridge polls at configurable cadence (`publish_interval_seconds=60.0` clamped to 1.0 minimum), posts to Ward Room `system_observability` channel via `WardRoomService.create_post`, emits `OBSERVABILITY_SNAPSHOT_PUBLISHED` per cycle. `_publish_once` traps `create_post` exceptions and emits `OBSERVABILITY_BRIDGE_FAILED` (log-and-degrade tier; loop continues next cycle). 2 new EventTypes (`OBSERVABILITY_SNAPSHOT_PUBLISHED`, `OBSERVABILITY_BRIDGE_FAILED`), `ObservabilityBridgeConfig` (Pydantic; `enabled=True`, `publish_interval_seconds=60.0`, `system_channel="system_observability"`), public attributes `runtime.observability_bridge` + `runtime.observability_bridge_start_task` (Wave 5 convention #1 — both consumer-facing). v1 ships 3 of 7 capabilities; Hebbian-weight feed → AD-641a-i (needs public Hebbian read API), HXI surfaces → AD-641a-ii, Captain alert routing on threshold breach → AD-641a-iii, `AttentionManager.snapshot()` public API → AD-641a-iv (drops the `_queue` reach). 14 focused tests pass.

BF-257 CLOSED. DM Receive Rate Limiter — break ping-pong loops at the proactive receive layer. Two-layer sliding-window rate limiter on `ProactiveCognitiveLoop` (`proactive.py:146`): Layer 1 per-agent global budget (`dm_response_budget=6` per `dm_response_window_seconds=600.0` 10-minute window); Layer 2 per-pair bidirectional budget (`dm_pair_exchange_budget=8` per window — pair key is `":".join(sorted([a, b]))` so A->B and B->A share one counter). New `_dm_response_budget_exceeded(agent_id, partner_id, config)` synchronous in-memory check with lazy per-call pruning of expired timestamps. `_check_unread_dms` gated: Captain DMs (callsign-based check, case-insensitive — v1 acceptable per AD-499 ShipNamingPolicy + BF-244 callsign sync; identity-based check deferred to BF-257b once captain DID is canonical) bypass the gate; throttled DMs are NOT added to `_notified_dm_threads` so they retry after window expires (deferred-not-dropped semantics). Budget timestamps recorded after the gate passes and before routing. `dm_exchange_limit` lowered 40 -> 15 (BF-200 raised 5->40; 40 was excessive — 15 still allows substantive multi-agent DM threads). 4 existing tests updated (test_ad614 default-value assertion, test_bf200 method-name + audit-trail docstring, test_unread_dms fixture override + assertion). 14/14 focused tests pass; 184/184 adjacent regression tests pass.

AD-449 CLOSED. MCP Bridge v1 OSS Infrastructure (Session + Client + Bridge + Adapter; EgressPolicy-gated). Created new `src/probos/integrations/` + `src/probos/integrations/mcp_bridge/` packages (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-457/459/466/467/475/469 precedents) with `MCPSession` (frozen dataclass — `server_url`/`session_id`/`headers`/`capabilities`; immutability via `dataclasses.replace`), `MCPClient` (JSON-RPC 2.0 over Streamable HTTP via `httpx.AsyncClient`; **`_last_response_headers` is INSTANCE attribute initialized in `__init__`** per Wave 8 second-pass review — class-level mutable default would race across MCPClient instances; `initialize`/`list_tools`/`call_tool` public API; `_call` gates URL via `runtime.egress_policy.is_allowed(url)` before any HTTP issuance — emits `MCP_BRIDGE_FAILED` with `reason="egress_blocked"` then raises `MCPProtocolError`; HTTP non-2xx and JSON-RPC `error` payloads emit `MCP_BRIDGE_FAILED` with `reason="http_error"`/`reason="rpc_error"`), `MCPBridge` (server registry — `register_server` rejects empty/duplicate URLs; `invoke(server_url, tool, args)` raises `MCPProtocolError` on unknown server; routes to per-server `MCPClient`), `MCPToolAdapter` (`name` property — `mcp.<sanitized_host>.<tool>` format from `urllib.parse.urlparse`; `__call__` proxies to bridge; ToolRegistry registration deferred to AD-449b per Solution Overview). 2 new EventTypes (`MCP_BRIDGE_INVOKE`, `MCP_BRIDGE_FAILED`), `MCPConfig` + `MCPServerConfig` (Pydantic; `enabled=True`, `request_timeout_seconds=30.0`, `servers: list[MCPServerConfig] = []`), public attribute `runtime.mcp_bridge`. **Commercial-boundary discipline:** OSS bridge infrastructure ships in v1; vendor-specific MCP server packs live in the private commercial repo entirely (AD-450 leak precedent). v1 ships 2 of 6 capabilities; ProbOS-as-MCP-server (Tool Registry registration via SkillCatalog) → AD-449b, MCP `sampling/createMessage` callback → AD-449c, OAuth 2.1 + DCR + dynamic scope → AD-449d, multi-tenant audit + observability → AD-449e. 14 focused tests pass.

Wave 5-8 architect decisions 2026-04-30: **AD-443 deferred** from Wave 5 to Wave 9C (or later) — mobility protocol's `verify_remote_chain` and slot-reassignment paths require AD-479 federation infrastructure that hasn't shipped; building in Wave 5 would land a half-feature. Wave 5 fifth slot is AD-499 (Ship & Crew Naming Conventions). **AD-641 split** scheduled as Wave 8.5 meta-prompt — umbrella ADs (AD-450, AD-462, AD-641) consistently produce poor builds when absorbed directly; AD-641 splits into 641a/b/f (Wave 9A, parallel-safe), 641c/e (Wave 9B, cross-cutting), 641d (Wave 9C, high-risk). Convention adopted: **umbrella ADs must be split into sub-AD prompts before scheduling, never built directly.** See `prompts/WAVE-5-8-RECONCILED-PLAN.md`.

Combo A CLOSED. 7 trivial extensions in single commit (AD-538b/572b/573b/576b/526c/655/656). AD-526c: `recreation/metadata.py` `GameMetadata` dataclass; `register_engine(engine, metadata=None)` extended with optional metadata + emits `RECREATION_GAME_REGISTERED`; `default_game` Captain-preference property; existing `register_engine`/`get_available_games`/`_engines` preserved (no DRY violation). AD-655: `EpisodicMemory.retrieve_contrastive_episodes(query, k)` returns mid-band `[0.4, 0.65]` similarity episodes; emits `CONTRASTIVE_RECALL`; `EvaluateHandler.__call__` populates `context["_contrastive_priors"]` when `runtime.episodic_memory` is wired. AD-656: `DepartmentCognitiveProfile` + `DepartmentProfilesConfig` Pydantic classes; `EvaluateHandler.__call__` overrides recall depth from per-department profile; emits `DEPT_PROFILE_APPLIED`. **AD-575b dropped wholesale** (theater per convention #7): `runtime.self_summary_provider` does not exist; both halves of AD-575b would have been permanent no-ops. Awaits a future AD that ships the upstream surface with a real consumer. 5 new EventTypes (`DREAM_MANIFEST_UPDATED`, `CAPTAIN_DM_PRIORITY_QUEUED`, `RECREATION_GAME_REGISTERED`, `CONTRASTIVE_RECALL`, `DEPT_PROFILE_APPLIED`). Public attributes `runtime.dream_manifest` + `runtime.captain_engagement_provider`. 26 focused tests pass.

AD-456 CLOSED. Security Infrastructure (Secrets / Egress / Audit). Extended existing `CredentialStore` (AD-395 at `credential_store.py:32`) with kw-only `store_path` and `emit_event` constructor kwargs, four additive methods (`_load_store`, `_resolve_from_store`, `rotate`, `_emit_rotated`), and a JSON-store step in `_resolve` between env-var aliases and CLI fallback. Atomic write via `os.replace`; rotation skipped when env var precedes (preserving AD-395 priority). Emits `SECRET_ROTATED` with `source` (`store`/`env`/`no_store`) + `persisted` flag. Created `src/probos/security/egress.py` (`EgressPolicy` + `EgressDecision`; `deny_by_default=True` default with allowlist `127.0.0.1`/`localhost`/`::1`; emits `EGRESS_BLOCKED` on every blocked check — real signal today, no theater) and `src/probos/security/audit.py` (`AuditLog` + `AuditEntry`; SHA-256 hash chain with GENESIS_HASH; `verify_chain()` detects mid-chain and genesis tampering; emits `AUDIT_RECORDED` per append). 3 new EventTypes (`SECRET_ROTATED`, `EGRESS_BLOCKED`, `AUDIT_RECORDED`), `SecurityInfraConfig` (Pydantic), public attributes `runtime.egress_policy` + `runtime.audit_log` (existing `runtime.credential_store` reconfigured in finalize). No new `SecretsManager` class — folded into CredentialStore extension per Wave 7 revision. RuntimeSandbox deferred wholesale to AD-456b; SQLite-persisted audit deferred to AD-456d; HttpFetchAgent integration deferred to AD-456b. 16 focused tests pass.

Created `src/probos/cognitive/ground_truth.py` with `GroundTruthVerifier` (cross-references claimed completions against `runtime.work_item_store.get_booking_journal()` + `runtime.event_log.query()`; computes 4-signal score `journal_present`/`duration_nonzero`/`tokens_recorded`/`event_within_window`; threshold 0.75 inclusive; emits `VERIFICATION_PASSED`/`VERIFICATION_FAILED`) and `VerificationEpisodeWriter` (constructs typed `Episode` with `MemorySource.DIRECT.value` + verification metadata in `dag_summary["kind"]="ground_truth_verification"`; `importance=7` for failed verifications per AD-598; persists via `episodic_memory.store(episode)`). Added cross-layer `ALLOWED_EXCEPTIONS` entry for `cognitive/ground_truth.py → probos.workforce` (TYPE_CHECKING-only; runtime-injected via `runtime.work_item_store` — verified at `runtime.py:212`/`421`/`1532`). 2 new EventTypes (`VERIFICATION_PASSED`, `VERIFICATION_FAILED`), `GroundTruthConfig` (Pydantic), public attributes `runtime.ground_truth_verifier` + `runtime.verification_episode_writer`. v1 observes; active rejection deferred to AD-528b; trust-network feedback deferred to AD-528c; ReconciliationEscalator (AD-451) integration deferred. 14 focused tests pass.

AD-467 CLOSED. Operations Crew (Resource Allocation / Scheduling / Coordination). Created `src/probos/agents/operations/` package (owns directory creation; mirrors AD-455/457/459/466 precedents) with three `HeartbeatAgent` subclasses: `ResourceAllocatorAgent` (cross-pool capacity reporting via `pool.current_size` + `pool.target_size`; emits `RESOURCE_ALLOCATED` at configurable cadence — AD-466 review caught phantom `active_count`; corrected to real `@property` at `pool.py:53`), `SchedulerAgent` (per-task-kind cadenced emission of `TASK_SCHEDULED` events; default cadences: hourly audit / daily summary), `CoordinatorAgent` (event-driven `WORKFLOW_STARTED` emission with in-memory active-workflow registry; rejects duplicate names). Template/agent-type names disambiguated as `operations_<role>` (`operations_resource_allocator`, `operations_scheduler`, `operations_coordinator`) to avoid collision with the existing bundled cognitive `SchedulerAgent` (utility CognitiveAgent registered at `runtime.py:599`); template registration uses `as Ops*` aliases inside `__init__`. 3 new EventTypes (`RESOURCE_ALLOCATED`, `TASK_SCHEDULED`, `WORKFLOW_STARTED`), `OperationsConfig` (Pydantic). v1 ships only the 3 agents; WorkflowDefinition API → AD-467b, Response-Time Scaling → AD-467c, LLM Cost Tracker (depends on AD-463 ModelRegistry MODEL_ROUTED payload) → AD-467d. 14 focused tests pass.

AD-463 CLOSED. Model Diversity & Neural Routing — Foundation. Created `src/probos/cognitive/model_registry.py` (`ModelDescriptor` frozen dataclass with provider/tier/capabilities/cost/context-window fields; `ModelRegistry` in-memory catalog seeded with 3 default descriptors — `gpt-4o-mini`/`claude-sonnet-4-6`/`claude-opus-4-0`; `register`/`get`/`by_tier`/`all`/`mark_unavailable`/`mark_available` public API) and `src/probos/cognitive/model_router.py` (`ModelRouter` consults registry; cost-aware cheapest-by-output-cost selection with optional `cost_ceiling` filter; emits `MODEL_ROUTED`/`MODEL_FALLBACK`; cross-tier fallback when no candidates available). Real consumer hook: `OpenAICompatibleClient.__init__` now accepts kw-only `model_router=None`; `_resolve_model_for_tier()` helper consults the router via defensive `getattr(..., None)` (handles tests that bypass `__init__` via `__new__`); `_complete_inner` line 442-447 SEARCH/REPLACE inserts `_override = self._resolve_model_for_tier(attempt_tier); model = _override or tc["model"]` — caller-optional, preserves existing tier-default path when router absent. 2 new EventTypes (`MODEL_ROUTED`, `MODEL_FALLBACK`), `ModelRoutingConfig` (Pydantic with optional `cost_ceiling_per_million_output_tokens`), public attributes `runtime.model_registry` + `runtime.model_router` + `client.model_router` (no leading underscore).
