BF-250 CLOSED. Resolved by `_make_mock_runtime` AsyncMock hardening for ward_room methods. Root cause: `MagicMock(spec=WardRoomService)` does not auto-wrap async methods as AsyncMock; `rt.ward_room.get_unread_dms()` was awaited as a regular MagicMock, which on Windows ProactorEventLoop yields to the event loop and blocks indefinitely on `_overlapped.GetQueuedCompletionStatus`. Test now passes under `-n 16 --dist=loadfile` and `-n 0`.

BF-251 CLOSED. Same root cause as BF-250 — `_run_cycle` calls `_check_unread_dms` BEFORE the BF-023 try/except around `_think_for_agent`, so the unawaitable MagicMock hung before the RuntimeError side_effect was reached. Resolved by the same mock hardening.

After AD-680 promoted `emergence_metrics_engine` to a public property on `ProbOSRuntime`, `finalize._populate_agent_tiers` reads the public name; `SimpleNamespace` does not expose `@property` descriptors, so the wiring was silently skipped and the assertion `emergence.registry is runtime._tier_registry` failed. Fix: PostBudget dataclass threaded from process_and_post() → extract_and_execute_actions() → _extract_and_execute_replies(). Budget spent on first create_post; subsequent [REPLY]/[MOVE] blocks and Step 7 main post suppressed. Telemetry event on suppression. Diagnostic logging. Crew-originated (Vance, Chapel, Anvil, Reed). 7 new tests. Issue #338.
BF-236 CLOSED. Semantic duplicate dispatch — round-scoped post tracker. `_route_to_agents()` had no check for "agent already posted in this conversational round." Fix: `_posted_in_round` tracker on WardRoomRouter, keyed on `(agent_id, thread_id)`, cleared on Captain repost. Recorded by WardRoomPostPipeline after `create_post`. 120s eviction. Gate in Phase 1 after Layer 3, respects `is_direct_target` bypass. 13 new tests.
BF-234 CLOSED. Duplicate ward room responses — consumer-side dedup. 4 of 12 agents posted duplicate replies due to transport-layer duplicate delivery (JetStream redelivery or js_publish timeout-then-succeed). Fix: `_seen_intents` dict in `IntentBus._on_dispatch()` — 300s sliding-window keyed by `intent.id`, filtered to `ward_room_notification` only. WARN log + counter + telemetry event. `msg.ack()` on hit. BF-198 remains semantic round-tracking only. 11 new tests.
AD-556 CLOSED. Adaptive trust anomaly detection — per-agent z-score thresholding. Rolling window of per-agent trust score history (default 30 snapshots). Z-score computed against agent's personal delta baseline. Debounce requires consecutive anomalous cycles (default 2) before escalation. Dual-gate: population sigma AND personal z-score must both fire. New agents without history fall back to population-only detection. Four new config parameters on EmergentDetectorConfig. 26 new tests. Crew-originated design (Forge + Reyes, 2026-04-01).
BF-233 CLOSED. BF-204 grounding check false positives — entity IDs from ward room params (thread_id, channel_id, author_id) and agent identity (_agent_id, intent_id) not included in grounding source, causing legitimate ward room replies to be suppressed as confabulations. Fix: expand grounding source to include all entity IDs the agent was explicitly given in its input context. Fabricated hex IDs still caught. Known limitation: cross-agent post UUID references (not in agent's own params) may still trigger if agent uses full UUID instead of truncated 8-char form. 11 new tests.
BF-232 CLOSED. ensure_stream() inherits stale subject filters from previous boot — update_stream() silently fails to change subjects (BF-231 finding), causing persistent "no response from stream" warnings on every JetStream publish. Fix: new recreate_stream() method uses delete-then-create; ensure_stream() unchanged (non-destructive). Phase 2 startup and set_subject_prefix() now use recreate_stream(). _delete_stream() failure logging promoted from DEBUG to WARNING for real failures (not-found stays DEBUG). Completes BF-229/230/231 NATS resilience trilogy. 8 new tests.

Four reflection sources: convergence reports, emergence snapshots, notebook consolidations, dominant cluster patterns. MemorySource.REFLECTION enum value. AD-601 CLOSED. TCM Temporal Context Vectors — Howard & Kahana (2002) Temporal Context Model for episodic memory. Drifting context vector (d=16, rho=0.95) stored alongside each episode. Cosine similarity between current and stored context provides smooth temporal proximity gradient, replacing binary watch_section matching for new episodes. Legacy episodes fall back to BF-147/BF-155 logic. Pure Python, no numpy/scipy. 23 tests.

Depends on AD-595d.

Intent, outcome, and department question templates. Reflection NOT templated into questions (grammar issues). No LLM call. 15 new tests. Completes AD-584 recall improvement wave.
BF-230 CLOSED. JetStream publish silently drops events under CPU load — no retry, no fallback to core NATS. Fix: js_publish() retries once (0.5s backoff), then falls back to core NATS publish, only ERROR if both fail. Configurable timeout (default 5s) via NatsConfig.js_publish_timeout. 6 new tests. Issue #335.

Fix: `records_store` forwarded through init_dreaming() constructor (available Phase 4→5). `ward_room` and `get_department` get public setter methods (genuinely unavailable until Phase 7). finalize.py now calls setters instead of reaching into private attrs. 8 new tests. Issue #19.
BF-229 CLOSED. NATS subject prefix rejects colons from ship DID — JetStream streams stranded with probos.local.* filters after Phase 7 commissioning sets prefix to probos.did:probos:<uuid>. All js_publish() calls fail with "no response from stream". Fix: NATSBus.set_subject_prefix() sanitizes NATS-unsafe chars to underscores (boundary enforcement); ensure_stream() re-raises after logging; stream update failure promoted to ERROR. 5 new tests + 1 existing test updated. Issue #334.
BF-228 CLOSED. Communications Array Degraded/Restored flapping — proactive loop transitions to "degraded" on 1 failure, resets on next success, flooding All Hands with Bridge Alerts. Three fixes: (1) raised degraded threshold from >=1 to >=3 (hysteresis, matches LLM client _UNREACHABLE_THRESHOLD), (2) only count actual LLM errors (timeout/connection/rate-limit) not generic agent failures, (3) decay failure count on success instead of hard-reset to 0. Issue #332.

Issue #243) | AD-633 SCOPED (Predictive Cognitive Branching — umbrella AD enabling crew agents to engage in forward-looking thought during idle cycles. Three functions: (1) Pre-Computation (CPU branch prediction analog — speculate on likely sub-tasks, serve pre-computed results), (2) Anticipatory Reasoning (Prospection/DMN analog — forward-looking thought producing new awareness, skill gap identification, PROPOSAL generation), (3) Goal Origination (Constructive Episodic Simulation/Preplay analog — agents generate novel goals from observation). Four speculation tiers: Zero-Cost deterministic, Cheap fast-tier >70%, Standard >85%, Anticipatory self-originated idle. Separate speculative token budget (not competing with operational). Prediction accuracy feedback loop (flush rate >30% = budget reduction). Earned Agency gates budgets by trust tier. Preplay dream Step 13 (forward simulation + implementation intentions). Prediction errors feed AD-557 EmergenceMetrics as novelty signal. Repeated predictions compile into Cognitive JIT procedures. Nine sub-ADs: 633a prediction engine, 633b executor+cache, 633c budget management, 633d pipeline integration, 633e accuracy tracking, 633f anticipatory reasoning, 633g preplay dream step, 633h emergence signal, 633i Cognitive JIT compilation. Research: docs/research/predictive-cognitive-branching.md (14 sources: Seligman, Schacter, DMN, Dragoi, Friston, Gollwitzer, Ha/Schmidhuber). Depends: AD-632, AD-531-539, AD-557, AD-573, AD-357. ## Release Tagging Policy

Tags use the format `v{major}.{minor}.{patch}-phase{N}` (e.g., `v0.4.0-phase29c`).

| Event | Tag? | Example |
|-------|------|---------|
| Phase completed | Yes — bump minor version | `v0.5.0-phase30` |
| Critical bug fix between phases | Yes — bump patch version | `v0.4.1-phase29c` |
| Roadmap/docs-only changes | No | — |
| Mid-phase work (partial features) | No | — |
| First production-ready release | Yes — `v1.0.0` | After security hardening (Phase 31) |

**Current tags:**

| Tag | Commit | Date | Notes |
|-----|--------|------|-------|
| `v0.1.0-phase12` | — | — | End of Era I: Genesis |
| `v0.4.0-phase29c` | `13d52a7` | 2026-03-17 | End of Phase 29c: Codebase Knowledge |

## Design Principles

See [design-principles.md](docs/development/design-principles.md) for full design principles:
- "Brains are Brains" (Nooplex core principle)
- Agent Development Model (Communication + Simulation)
- HXI Self-Sufficiency, Agent-First Design, Cockpit View
- Probabilistic Agents, Consensus Governance
- Agent Classification Framework (Core / Utility / Domain)
- Foundational Governance Axioms (Safety Budget, Reversibility, Minimal Authority)
- "Cooperate, Don't Compete" (Federation philosophy)
- Visiting Officer Subordination
- Extension-First Architecture
- Markdown is Code

## Environment

- **Platform:** Windows 11 Pro (10.0.26200)
- **Python:** 3.12.13 (installed via uv)
- **Toolchain:** uv 0.10.9
- **Key deps:** pydantic 2.12.5, pyyaml 6.0.3, aiosqlite 0.22.1, httpx 0.28+, rich 13.0+, chromadb 1.5.4, pytest 9.0.2, pytest-asyncio 1.3.0, pytest-xdist 3.8.0, pytest-timeout 2.4.0
- **LLM endpoints:** Fast tier: Ollama at `http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1`, Standard/Deep tier: VS Code Copilot proxy at `http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1`
- **LLM models:** fast=qwen3.5:35b (local Ollama), standard=claude-sonnet-4.6 (Copilot proxy), deep=claude-opus-4.6 (Copilot proxy)
- **Run tests:** `uv run pytest tests/ -v` (sequential) or `uv run pytest -n auto` (parallel, 13x faster)
- **Run fast tests:** `uv run pytest -n auto -m "not slow"` (skip 65 sleep-heavy tests)
- **Run demo:** `uv run python demo.py`
- **Run interactive:** `uv run python -m probos`

- AD-730 - vision pipe-through for per-agent DMs shipped (Wave 151, Python +N), then partially compromised by BF-265 transport-strip. Resolved by AD-731 (Wave 152) — see below.

- BF-265 - IntentBus strip of `vision_messages` from NATS transport shipped Wave 151 as emergency fix for #636 OOM crash (1 MB allocation failure from inline-base64 retry-buffer accumulation). **REVERTED in Wave 152 / AD-731** — content-addressable refs make the strip unnecessary; the uniform-NATS-transport invariant is restored.

- BF-266 - fold full `user_message` into vision DM multimodal content shipped (Wave 151). Behavior unchanged by AD-731; the enrichment is shape-agnostic (matches on `type == 'image'`).

- BF-267 - IntentBus.send() local-first dispatch shipped Wave 151, **REVERTED 2026-05-11** (commit 8b4b39f) because the local handler is async and returns immediately (cognitive queue enqueue), so `await handler(intent)` returned `None` — broke ALL DMs including text. Architecturally the local-first reflex was the wrong-direction fix; the right fix is the wire format (AD-731).

- AD-731 (Wave 152) - content-addressable vision payloads shipped (Python +13 = 13342). Sender (`build_multimodal_messages`) emits `{type:image, source:{type:attachment_ref, sha256, media_type}}` instead of inline base64. Receiver (`OpenAICompatibleClient._resolve_attachment_refs_for_openai`) dereferences refs from the runtime's content-addressable `AttachmentStore` to base64 immediately before the HTTP POST. Bus message stays ~70 bytes/image (was 150 KB-1 MB). BF-265 reverted as a consequence — AD-637z2 (#639) auto-closes. Federation strip preserved as an explicit AD-731a forward marker (cross-mesh attachment distribution unsolved; AD-731a-1 HTTP fetch and AD-731a-2 NATS Object Store will retire it). Industry pattern (refs not bytes on RPC) matches Ray/Dask object refs, Erlang BEAM refs, MCP resource handles, Anthropic API source types, Git, IPFS, OCI registries.

- BF-264 closeout (Wave 154) - the 2026-05-11 OOM crash (1 MB allocation failure after dream cycle) is retroactively resolved by AD-731's wire-format change — vision payloads no longer carry image bytes through `IntentMessage.params`; only ~70-byte refs cross the bus. The original BF-264 hypothesis list (divergence ring buffers, WS push snapshots, NATS retry buffers) remains useful as **ambient health watch-points** for future memory-pressure investigations, not as open work. Closes [#636](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/636).


- AD-734 (Wave 153) - vision-pipeline wire-shape contract test shipped (Python +1 test file, 3 tests, ~230 lines). Codifies the live-capture work done with `tmp_capture_proxy.py` during BF-278 as a CI-runnable pytest at `tests/test_ad734_wire_shape_contract.py`. Pins three invariants: (1) sender bus shape `{type:image, source:{type:attachment_ref,...}}` per AD-731, (2) resolver rewrites to OpenAI `image_url` + data URL per BF-268, (3) end-to-end `_call_openai` HTTP POST body via `httpx.MockTransport` carries `image_url` (not Anthropic source-shape). Pre-commit hook (`.git/hooks/pre-commit`, +~20 lines) runs the contract test whenever any vision-pipeline file is staged — would have caught BF-274 and BF-278 at commit time. Lesson locked in: contract tests on the wire boundary, not just on component APIs, are the durable guard against silent multi-subsystem regressions.

- AD-738 (Wave 157) — Server-streamed TTS via Piper shipped (Python +26 tests, Vitest +7). Closes forward marker AD-721b-2.3. MIT-licensed operator-provided binary (`tools/piper/`) + MIT default voice model (`en_US-amy-medium`). New `src/probos/audio/tts/` module with `TTSBackend` Protocol + `PiperBackend` subprocess wrapper. New `TTSConfig` Pydantic model with `backend = "browser"` default (zero behaviour change for operators who don't install Piper). New `GET /api/avatars/tts/status` probe + `POST /api/avatars/tts` synthesis endpoint; AD-731 invariant (audio bytes via `AttachmentStore.write(sha256, blob, mime)`, no inline base64 in RPC body). Browser caches one-time probe in `voice.ts` module state — load-bearing zero-HTTP-per-utterance guarantee on default config. Honest-degrade chain: any failure (binary missing, model missing, subprocess error, timeout, malformed output) returns `{"backend": "disabled"}` and browser falls back to `SpeechSynthesisUtterance`. `useLipSyncCapture` extended with `injectLipSyncFrames` setter — server path injects visemes alongside audio for lockstep playback. AD-735 volume + AD-737 emotion modulation apply to BOTH paths. Forward markers AD-738a/b/c/d filed in roadmap.

----

AD-728d v1 CLOSED (Wave 165). Self-image-awareness skill (LLM-discoverable self-check capability). Closes GH issue #661. Wraps AD-728c's `CognitiveAgent.check_own_render(reason)` coroutine in the canonical AD-626 augmentation-skill pattern so agents can invoke render self-checks via their LLM reasoning surface. New `config/skills/self-image-awareness/SKILL.md` (activation=augmentation, intents=direct_message,ward_room_notification,proactive_think, trigger tag self_check) teaches the `[SELF_CHECK reason]` bracket marker. New `_SELF_CHECK_RE` (strict `[a-z_-]{1,64}` reason — dispatch authority) + `_SELF_CHECK_STRIP_RE` (lax leak-prevention) + `extract_self_check` / `strip_self_check` helpers on `DmSanityGate`, positioned adjacent to the CHALLENGE/MOVE regex pairs. New `DmReplyPipeline.step_4_self_check_parse` inserted between move-parse and episodic-store; trailing five step defs renumbered 5..9 atomically (step tuple + 5 method defs + class docstring + module docstring in one commit). Fire-and-forget `asyncio.Task` reference held on `DmReplyContext._self_check_task` per fire-and-forget GC rule (not on agent — tests verify storage location). First marker dispatches `agent.check_own_render(reason=...)`; additional markers in the same reply are stripped silently with a single WARNING. Invalid reasons (uppercase, punctuation) silently strip with no dispatch. Disabled-gate path still dispatches — honest-degrade lives downstream in `verify_render_coherence` (AD-728c surface). 7 focused tests pass at `tests/test_ad728d_self_image_awareness_skill.py`: SKILL.md frontmatter loads with correct intents AND negative-intent assertion (skill-catalog-leak hard-stop); `find_augmentation_skills` surfaces the skill for the three configured intents AND does NOT surface for unrelated intents (system_heartbeat, run_command, http_fetch); marker stripped from reply text; valid reason dispatches with task ref held on ctx; invalid reason silently strips with no dispatch; multi-marker first-dispatches + WARNING logged; disabled-gate still dispatches. Real `DmSanityGate` fixture + hand-rolled `_RecordingAgent` dataclass (NOT `unittest.mock`) per BF-287. AD-726 `test_ad726_dm_reply_pipeline.py` renumbered alongside (step_4_episodic_store → step_5, etc.; `len(reached) == 7` → `8`). Full gate: 13769 passed, 20 skipped (delta +7 vs Wave 164 baseline 13762; 1 flake on `test_knowledge_store::test_auto_commit_after_debounce` passes in isolation). AD-731 invariant preserved — text-only marker; grep `base64` in `reply_pipeline.py` empty. AD-728c boundary preserved — `check_own_render` body unchanged, rate-limit logic unchanged, observation injection unchanged. No new pip/npm deps. No hard-stops triggered.
